{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"You are an INDEPENDENT adversarial security reviewer (a second, separate pass from the primary red-pen). Repo: /home/kali/sigil, branch phase8-ws-g-delegate. Target: the DELEGATE web-actor (SIGIL Phase 8 WS-G), the highest-risk feature in the system — it manages the owner's credentials and takes outbound web actions (create account / login / submit forms). Find real defects with concrete reproductions. Do NOT fix anything; report only. Assume the primary reviewer will cover the obvious lenses — YOUR job is to find what they miss: subtle, second-order, and integration bugs.\n\nStart by reading the commit: `cd /home/kali/sigil && git show 2b713a0`. Then read these to understand the substrate: sigil/agents/actor.py, vault.py, actor_gate.py, actor_scope.py, web_engine.py, operator.py, approvals.py, base.py, sources.py, governor/promotion.py, governor/__init__.py (the Governor.decide gate), spine/store.py (append/get/iter_records semantics), and sigil/mesh/__init__.py (authorized_devices).\n\nYou MAY run code to prove a defect: use ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python to write a throwaway repro script (put it in the scratchpad, not the repo) that constructs a SpineStore(tempfile), a FakeEngine, a fake vault, an ActorScope, a Delegate, generate_keypair() for an owner, ApprovalQueue for approvals, and demonstrates the bad outcome. A working repro that violates doctrine is worth far more than a hypothesis.\n\nHunt specifically in these under-examined areas:\n- GOVERNOR INTERACTION: Delegate.preview dispatches an A3 Proposal. Trace Governor.decide(agent=DELEGATE, tier=A3, ceiling=A2, scope=\"event\"). Does it QUEUE (correct) or could a promotion/kill-switch/budget state make it AUTO-apply or behave unexpectedly? Since scope is the record KIND \"event\" (generic), could a promotion granted for some OTHER agent's \"event\" scope leak into DELEGATE? (DELEGATE is NO_PROMOTION — verify that actually blocks is_promoted for the \"event\" scope, not just scope \"*\".)\n- SCOPE OF THE ACTION_TOKEN vs THE QUEUED RECORD: action_approved checks rec.payload['signal']==ACTION_SIGNAL and rec.payload['action_token']==token, then finds an approval with target_seq==seq. But the queued record's payload is written by base._dispatch which MERGES the proposal payload with {'decision':'queued','status':'awaiting-approval','agent','tier','governor'}. Confirm action_token survives that merge intact and that nothing lets an attacker mint a record with signal==ACTION_SIGNAL + a chosen action_token + a matching self-approval. Can a DIFFERENT agent or a raw store.append forge an ACTION_SIGNAL 'applied' record to fake success or poison creation_allowed's count?\n- CREATION-CAP COUNTING: actor_scope.creation_allowed counts records where signal=='web.actor.step' AND step_kind=='account.create' AND status=='applied'. Note the QUEUED record also has signal=='web.actor.step' and step_kind but status=='awaiting-approval' (not 'applied'). Verify the count is right. THEN check the multi-preview race: preview 3 account.create steps (0 applied → all 3 queue), approve all 3, execute all 3. Does execute re-check creation_allowed? Read actor.execute end to end. If it does not, PROVE a cap=1 bypass with a runnable script (3 applied account.create records for one service).\n- JOURNAL PATH: the queued spine record stores payload['journal'] = a path string. execute() reads that path with Path(...).read_text(). Is the path attacker-influenceable (it's derived from the action_token, written under a 0700 dir)? Could a crafted service/kind/url produce a token collision or a path-traversal in the filename? (token is sha256 hex — argue whether that's safe.) What if two different steps hash to journal files and one overwrites the other's content?\n- FIELD RESOLUTION: _resolve maps 'vault:email'/'vault:username'/'vault:password'/'literal:...'. What happens for an UNKNOWN source string (e.g. 'vault:secret_question' or 'env:PATH' or 'file:/etc/passwd')? Does it silently drop (safe) or leak/err? What if fields is empty, or a value is not a string? What if a literal value itself starts with 'vault:'?\n- BLOCK DETECTION COMPLETENESS/HONESTY: detect_block keys on status 403/429 and a regex over html. Is the claim \"a block STOPS + is surfaced\" honest given detect_block might MISS a block (e.g. a 200 soft-block page with no captcha keyword, or a JS-challenge)? That's an over-claim risk, not a security hole per se — flag if the code or comments over-promise. Also: does needs_js exist but go unused (dead code implying a browser path that isn't there)? Is that honest?\n- HttpEngine.act wire details: it urlencodes `resolved` into the POST body. Confirm credentials go ONLY in the body, never the URL/query, never a logged string, never an exception message. Check the timeout/finally/exception handling doesn't stringify the body. Confirm no Referer is set and the UA is the fixed correlatable one (not rotated).\n- The vault: set_record writes the manifest with os.open(0600) but does _save chmod the file if it already exists with looser perms? Does _load tolerate a corrupted/hostile manifest? Can version go backwards to defeat rotation-binding?\n\nFor every real defect: state severity, exact file:line, a concrete repro (ideally a runnable script and its actual output), the doctrine violated, and a fix direction. Rank by severity. If the strongest attacks all fail, say precisely what you tried and why the design held. Be concrete and honest — a confirmed low-severity bug beats a hand-wavy \"maybe critical\"."},"uuid":"a2d49068-7f37-4765-8ed0-a0447ac8f0d7","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:05:57.210Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"d4a0a1d4-c9be-4a1c-ac30-56d4ccfd1465","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- crucible: How to operate CRUCIBLE — the authorized, prove-don't-guess offensive-security engine in framework/v2. Read this BEFORE running an engagement/scan, re-verifying a finding, inspecting the event spine, reasoning about the veracity/anti-hallucination layer, the learning/metacognition core, or the safety stack. Use it when a task names CRUCIBLE, OBSIDIAN, `python3 -m framework.v2 <subcommand>`, findings/oracles/evidence certificates, the blackboard event spine, critics, reflection, or the reward/calibration loop.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- adversarial-sweep: Independent multi-lens adversarial re-attack of a defensive-oracle domain — the FORGE stage-9 companion to RED-PEN. Attack from disjoint lenses, refute each objection with a real repro, report only what survives. - Before merging any AEGIS/FORGE domain stream, alongside (not instead of) the red-pen agent. Pass the domain spec via args. On the email-auth stream it caught 3 CRITICAL/LOW defects PAST a RED-PEN PASS — near-zero-FP cannot be self-certified by one reviewer.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":19,"isInitial":true,"names":["crucible","deep-research","adversarial-sweep","dataviz","artifact-design","artifact-capabilities","update-config","keybindings-help","verify","code-review","simplify","fewer-permission-prompts","loop","schedule","claude-api","run","init","review","security-review"]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"ea26508a-3b26-4ebe-ba4b-50e211b9d1da","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:05:57.225Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Offense-free BY CONSTRUCTION:\n    \n      - credential vault holds NO password field (keyring ref only); the password\n        resolves from the keyring into a local var at the last instant of execute,\n        never on the append-only spine, the 0700 step journal, a log, or a payload.\n      - the executable step (incl. any literal values) lives in a 0700 journal off\n        the spine; the spine binds by service + page-hash + a value-free field\n        binding of NAMES + vault-refs + literal-content-hashes + the vault VERSION.\n      - transaction: preview (A0 dry-run GET, block-detect, redacted A3 queue) ->\n        per-action owner-signed approve bound to action_token -> execute (re-fetch,\n        re-derive token: page change OR credential rotation OR journal edit aborts;\n        block-before-act; resolve creds last-instant; act; record result only).\n      - a CAPTCHA/403/429 STOPS and is surfaced as a positive control; there is NO\n        browser-escalation code path in the actor at all (HTTP-only), so 'browser to\n        beat a block' is structurally unreachable.\n      - per-service creation cap (mass creation out of doctrine); ActorScope origin\n        allowlist (deny-all) + SSRF gate; NO as_identity/impersonate parameter.\n    \n    Files: agents/{actor,vault,actor_gate,actor_scope,web_engine}.py (vault/gate/\n    scope/engine landed earlier this branch), agents/actor.py (the agent), +\n    governor/promotion.py (DELEGATE -> NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS), agents/__init__.py.\n    tests/test_actor.py: 16/16. Manages the\n2\towner's OWN credentials and, with PER-ACTION owner approval, creates accounts / logs in / fills forms /\n3\tsubmits on the owner's behalf. Ceiling A2, and added to NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS — every account/login/\n4\tsubmit/purchase is A3 (explicit, per-action, owner-signed, no promotion; the WARDEN oracle forces this\n5\tfrom the honest tool name). Fuses the Operator transaction (plan → preview-by-reference →\n6\ttoken-bound-approve → re-derive-at-execute → verify) with the egress per-action token.\n7\t\n8\tOffense-free by construction:\n9\t  • NO `as_identity`/impersonate parameter exists — fields resolve ONLY from the owner's OWN vault.\n10\t  • a CAPTCHA / 403 / 429 STOPS and is surfaced as a positive control; the block-check precedes any\n11\t    action, and there is NO browser-escalation code path in the actor at all (HTTP-only), so \"use a\n12\t    browser to beat a block\" is structurally unreachable.\n13\t  • per-service creation cap (mass creation is out of doctrine); `ActorScope` origin allowlist (deny-all).\n14\t  • credentials resolve from the keyring ONLY at the last instant of execute, into a local var — never a\n15\t    Proposal payload, never logged/journaled. The executable step (incl. any literal values) lives in a\n16\t    0700 journal OFF the append-only spine; the spine binds by service + page hash + a field_binding of\n17\t    NAMES + vault-references + literal-content-hashes + the vault VERSION (a page change OR a credential\n18\t    rotation OR a literal edit changes the token → the approval no longer verifies → re-approval).\"\"\"\n19\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n20\t\n21\timport json\n22\timport os\n23\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n24\tfrom pathlib import Path\n25\tfrom typing import List\n26\t\n27\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n28\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n29\tfrom .actor_gate import ACTION_SIGNAL, action_approved, action_token\n30\tfrom .actor_scope import ActorScope\n31\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n32\tfrom .web_engine import HttpEngine, detect_block\n33\t\n34\t_ACTOR_HOME = SIGIL_HOME / \"actor\"\n35\t_JOURNAL = _ACTOR_HOME / \"steps\"     # executable steps (WITH any literal values) — off the append-only spine\n36\t\n37\t# honest tool names — the WARDEN oracle forces each to A3 (explicit, per-action, no-promotion)\n38\t_TOOL = {\"navigate\": \"browser.navigate\", \"fill\": \"form.fill\", \"submit\": \"submit.form\",\n39\t         \"account.create\": \"account.create\", \"login\": \"login\", \"purchase\": \"purchase.item\"}\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef _secure(d: Path) -> None:\n43\t    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n44\t    try:\n45\t        os.chmod(d, 0o700)\n46\t    except OSError:\n47\t        pass\n48\t\n49\t\n50\tdef _redact_source(src: str) -> str:\n51\t    \"\"\"Spine-safe rendering of a field source: vault references pass through (they are references, not\n52\t    values); a literal is reduced to a content hash (the value is journaled 0700, never on the spine).\"\"\"\n53\t    if src.startswith(\"literal:\"):\n54\t        return \"literal:\" + sha256_hex(src[len(\"literal:\"):].encode(\"utf-8\"))[:16]\n55\t    return src\n56\t\n57\t\n58\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n59\tclass WebStep:\n60\t    service: str\n61\t    kind: str                                    # navigate|fill|submit|account.create|login|purchase\n62\t    url: str\n63\t    fields: dict = field(default_factory=dict)   # {form_field: \"vault:password\"|\"vault:email\"|\"vault:username\"|\"literal:...\"}\n64\t    # NOTE: there is deliberately NO identity/impersonate field — fields resolve ONLY from the owner's own vault.\n65\t\n66\t\n67\tclass Delegate(Agent):\n68\t    name = \"DELEGATE\"\n69\t    mandate = \"owner-consented identity/account manager + web actor; per-action approved, offense-free\"\n70\t    ceiling = Tier.A2\n71\t\n72\t    def __init__(self, store=None, *, scope: ActorScope, vault=None, engine=None,\n73\t                 classifier=None, trusted_pubkey=None):\n74\t        super().__init__(store)\n75\t        self.scope = scope\n76\t        self._vault = vault\n77\t        self.engine = engine or HttpEngine()\n78\t        self._classifier = classifier\n79\t        self._trusted = trusted_pubkey\n80\t\n81\t    def _vaultobj(self):\n82\t        if self._vault is None:\n83\t            from .vault import CredentialVault\n84\t            self._vault = CredentialVault()\n85\t        return self._vault\n86\t\n87\t    def _cls(self):\n88\t        if self._classifier is None:\n89\t            from .kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n90\t            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n91\t        return self._classifier\n92\t\n93\t    def _tp(self):\n94\t        if self._trusted is None:\n95\t            from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n96\t            self._trusted = owner_pubkey()\n97\t        return self._trusted\n98\t\n99\t    def _field_binding(self, service: str, fields: dict) -> str:\n100\t        \"\"\"A value-free binding: field NAMES + vault-references + literal-CONTENT-HASHES, prefixed with\n101\t        the current vault VERSION when any field references the vault (so a rotation → new token →\n102\t        re-approval). Deterministically reproducible at execute from the journaled fields + vault state.\"\"\"\n103\t        parts, uses_vault = [], False\n104\t        for k in sorted(fields):\n105\t            src = fields[k]\n106\t            if src.startswith(\"vault:\"):\n107\t                uses_vault = True\n108\t            parts.append(f\"{k}={_redact_source(src)}\")\n109\t        binding = \";\".join(parts)\n110\t        if uses_vault:\n111\t            rec = self._vaultobj().get_record(service)\n112\t            binding = f\"vaultv{rec.version if rec else 0}|{binding}\"\n113\t        return binding\n114\t\n115\t    # --- preview: dry-run GET, block-detect, journal off-spine, queue a per-action A3 (redacted) ---\n116\t    def preview(self, steps: List[WebStep]) -> tuple:\n117\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n118\t        queued: List[int] = []\n119\t        for step in steps:\n120\t            if not self.scope.origin_allowed(step.url):\n121\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n122\t                                  payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"requested\": step.url,\n123\t                                           \"reason\": \"target origin is not in the owner's ActorScope\"})\n124\t                res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED {step.url}: out of ActorScope\")\n125\t                continue\n126\t            if step.kind == \"account.create\" and not self.scope.creation_allowed(self.store, step.service):\n127\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n128\t                                  payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"service\": step.service,\n129\t                                           \"reason\": \"per-service creation cap (mass account creation is out of doctrine)\"})\n130\t                res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED account.create {step.service}: creation cap\")\n131\t                continue\n132\t            pv = self.engine.fetch(step.url)                 # A0 dry-run GET — no mutation\n133\t            block = detect_block(pv.status, pv.html)\n134\t            if block:                                        # STOP + surface — never defeat a block\n135\t                self.store.append(kind=\"finding\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n136\t                                  payload={\"signal\": \"web.actor.blocked\", \"decision\": \"surfaced\", \"url\": step.url,\n137\t                                           \"reason\": block, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n138\t                                           \"summary\": f\"{step.url} is blocked ({block}) — surfaced, NOT defeated\"})\n139\t                res.notes.append(f\"BLOCKED {step.url}: {block} — surfaced, not defeated (doctrine)\")\n140\t                continue\n141\t            page_hash = sha256_hex(pv.html.encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\"))\n142\t            fb = self._field_binding(step.service, step.fields)\n143\t            token = action_token(step.service, step.kind, page_hash, fb)\n144\t            _secure(_ACTOR_HOME)\n145\t            _secure(_JOURNAL)\n146\t            # executable step (WITH any literal values) → 0700 journal, keyed by token; NEVER the spine\n147\t            (_JOURNAL / f\"{token}.json\").write_text(json.dumps(\n148\t                {\"service\": step.service, \"kind\": step.kind, \"url\": step.url, \"fields\": step.fields}),\n149\t                encoding=\"utf-8\")\n150\t            tier = self._cls().classify(_TOOL.get(step.kind, \"unknown.op\"))   # → A3 for the mutating kinds\n151\t            r = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"event\", {\n152\t                \"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"service\": step.service, \"step_kind\": step.kind, \"url\": step.url,\n153\t                \"page_sha256\": page_hash, \"field_binding\": fb, \"action_token\": token,\n154\t                \"journal\": str(_JOURNAL / f\"{token}.json\"),\n155\t                \"fields_redacted\": [{\"name\": k, \"source\": _redact_source(step.fields[k])}\n156\t                                    for k in sorted(step.fields)],   # NAMES + refs/hashes only — NO values\n157\t                \"subject\": f\"{step.kind} on {step.service} (awaiting owner approval)\"}, tier)])\n158\t            seq = r.queued[0][\"seq\"] if r.queued else (r.applied[0] if r.applied else None)\n159\t            if seq is not None:\n160\t                queued.append(seq)\n161\t        return queued, res\n162\t\n163\t    # --- execute: verified approval → re-fetch → block-before-act → re-derive token → last-instant act\n164\t    def execute(self, step_seq: int) -> AgentResult:\n165\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n166\t        rec = self.store.get(step_seq)\n167\t        if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != ACTION_SIGNAL:\n168\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {step_seq} is not a web-actor step\")\n169\t            return res\n170\t        token = rec.payload[\"action_token\"]\n171\t        if not action_approved(self.store, step_seq, token, self._tp()):\n172\t            res.notes.append(f\"step {step_seq} has no verified owner approval — NO request made\")\n173\t            return res\n174\t        try:                                                 # executable step lives OFF the spine (0700)\n175\t            j = json.loads(Path(rec.payload[\"journal\"]).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n176\t            service, kind, url, fields = j[\"service\"], j[\"kind\"], j[\"url\"], j[\"fields\"]\n177\t        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n178\t            res.notes.append(\"step journal missing/unreadable — cannot execute\")\n179\t            return res\n180\t        pv = self.engine.fetch(url)                          # re-fetch (anti-TOCTOU + block-before-act)\n181\t        block = detect_block(pv.status, pv.html)\n182\t        if block:\n183\t            self.store.append(kind=\"finding\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n184\t                              payload={\"signal\": \"web.actor.blocked\", \"decision\": \"surfaced\", \"url\": url,\n185\t                                       \"reason\": block, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n186\t                                       \"summary\": f\"{url} blocked at execute ({block}) — surfaced, no action\"})\n187\t            res.notes.append(f\"BLOCKED at execute {url}: {block} — surfaced, no action\")\n188\t            return res\n189\t        # RE-DERIVE the token from the CURRENT page hash + CURRENT vault version + journaled fields; a\n190\t        # page change OR a credential rotation OR a journal edit → mismatch → abort (never trust a field).\n191\t        fresh = action_token(service, kind, sha256_hex(pv.html.encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")),\n192\t                             self._field_binding(service, fields))\n193\t        if fresh != token:\n194\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n195\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq,\n196\t                                       \"reason\": \"page/credential/step changed since approval (token mismatch) — aborted\"})\n197\t            res.notes.append(\"ABORTED: the world changed since approval (token mismatch) — no action\")\n198\t            return res\n199\t        resolved = self._resolve(service, fields)            # credentials → locals ONLY (never the spine)\n200\t        out = self.engine.act({\"url\": url, \"kind\": kind}, resolved)\n201\t        seq = self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n202\t                                payload={\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"status\": \"applied\", \"service\": service,\n203\t                                         \"step_kind\": kind, \"url\": url, \"action_token\": token,\n204\t                                         \"result_ok\": bool(out.get(\"ok\")), \"tier\": \"A1\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n205\t                                         \"summary\": f\"{kind} on {service} executed (ok={out.get('ok')})\"})\n206\t        res.applied.append(seq)\n207\t        res.notes.append(f\"executed {kind} on {service} (ok={out.get('ok')})\")\n208\t        return res\n209\t\n210\t    def _resolve(self, service: str, fields: dict) -> dict:\n211\t        \"\"\"Resolve field references to VALUES at the last instant — into a local dict, never the spine.\"\"\"\n212\t        out: dict = {}\n213\t        rec = self._vaultobj().get_record(service)\n214\t        for name, src in fields.items():\n215\t            if src == \"vault:password\":\n216\t                out[name] = self._vaultobj().resolve_password(service) or \"\"\n217\t            elif src == \"vault:email\":\n218\t                out[name] = rec.email if rec else \"\"\n219\t            elif src == \"vault:username\":\n220\t                out[name] = rec.username if rec else \"\"\n221\t            elif src.startswith(\"literal:\"):\n222\t                out[name] = src[len(\"literal:\"):]\n223\t        return out\n224\t"}]},"uuid":"b3c34609-4a9e-4ce6-85a7-4f4bd4bdd239","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:15.290Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5fde1c85-558b-4838-9f8d-162c3aae1386","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"b3c34609-4a9e-4ce6-85a7-4f4bd4bdd239","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHM6Ht5kbavcvD248sL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013mAm3bonLgm7t57hDoAam1","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/vault.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4803,"cache_read_input_tokens":32318,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4803,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHM5Ma9G1mcVRwnjLth","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"ccf57ede-b87d-4a6f-a10d-ce66793fd486","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:15.892Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"ccf57ede-b87d-4a6f-a10d-ce66793fd486","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013mAm3bonLgm7t57hDoAam1","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"CredentialVault (Phase 8, WS-G G-i) — the owner's OWN per-service credentials. Extends\n2\t`platform.secrets.SecretStore` preserving its invariant verbatim (\"Secrets NEVER enter the append-only\n3\tspine, a log, or a network payload\"). A `VaultRecord` has NO `password` field — only a `password_ref`\n4\t(a keyring key name); the password lives in the OS keyring. The manifest (email/username/ref/version)\n5\tlives in a 0700 dir OFF the append-only spine. The password is resolved from the keyring ONLY at\n6\texecute time, into a local variable — never assigned to a Proposal payload, never logged, never\n7\tjournaled. `version` bumps on every edit and BINDS an approval (rotate → version bump → re-approval),\n8\tso the spine binds by `service+vault_ref+version` — deliberately NOT a hash of the value (hashing a\n9\tlow-entropy identity field onto an append-only log is itself a weak-preimage leak).\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport json\n13\timport os\n14\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n15\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n18\t\n19\t_VAULT = SIGIL_HOME / \"vault\"\n20\t_MANIFEST = _VAULT / \"manifest.json\"\n21\t\n22\t\n23\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n24\tclass VaultRecord:\n25\t    service: str\n26\t    email: str = \"\"\n27\t    username: str = \"\"\n28\t    password_ref: str = \"\"            # keyring key name — NOT the password\n29\t    notes: str = \"\"\n30\t    version: int = 1\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tclass CredentialVault:\n34\t    def __init__(self, secret_store=None):\n35\t        from ..platform.secrets import SecretStore\n36\t        self.secrets = secret_store or SecretStore()\n37\t\n38\t    def _load(self) -> dict:\n39\t        try:\n40\t            return json.loads(_MANIFEST.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n41\t        except (OSError, ValueError):\n42\t            return {}\n43\t\n44\t    def _save(self, data: dict) -> None:\n45\t        _VAULT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n46\t        try:\n47\t            os.chmod(_VAULT, 0o700)\n48\t        except OSError:\n49\t            pass\n50\t        fd = os.open(str(_MANIFEST), os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)  # 0600 up-front\n51\t        with os.fdopen(fd, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n52\t            json.dump(data, f)\n53\t\n54\t    def set_record(self, service: str, *, email: str = \"\", username: str = \"\",\n55\t                   password: Optional[str] = None, notes: str = \"\") -> VaultRecord:\n56\t        ref = f\"vault/{service}/password\"\n57\t        if password is not None:\n58\t            self.secrets.set(ref, password)          # → keyring (or 0600 sigil.env), never the spine\n59\t        data = self._load()\n60\t        prev = data.get(service, {})\n61\t        rec = {\"service\": service, \"email\": email or prev.get(\"email\", \"\"),\n62\t               \"username\": username or prev.get(\"username\", \"\"), \"password_ref\": ref,\n63\t               \"notes\": notes or prev.get(\"notes\", \"\"), \"version\": int(prev.get(\"version\", 0)) + 1}\n64\t        data[service] = rec\n65\t        self._save(data)\n66\t        return VaultRecord(**rec)\n67\t\n68\t    def get_record(self, service: str) -> Optional[VaultRecord]:\n69\t        d = self._load().get(service)\n70\t        return VaultRecord(**d) if d else None\n71\t\n72\t    def records(self) -> List[VaultRecord]:\n73\t        return [VaultRecord(**d) for d in self._load().values()]\n74\t\n75\t    def resolve_password(self, service: str) -> Optional[str]:\n76\t        \"\"\"Fetch the password from the keyring — call ONLY at execute-time, into a local var.\"\"\"\n77\t        rec = self.get_record(service)\n78\t        return self.secrets.get(rec.password_ref) if rec else None\n79\t"}]},"uuid":"323ad771-6115-4610-bf41-c945fcf18f7d","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:15.902Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ccf57ede-b87d-4a6f-a10d-ce66793fd486","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"2f104626-edba-4f4f-8a66-d3cb68f1987c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TmXHqTEzEJqcKvuWRbX6W3","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Action gate (Phase 8, WS-G G-iii) — a near-verbatim mirror of `perception/egress.py`, but for web\n2\tACTIONS. `action_token = sha256(service|step|page_sha256|field_binding)` binds an approval to ONE\n3\texact action (a page-change or a vault-version bump changes the token → re-approval). `action_approved`\n4\trequires a VERIFIED owner/authorized-device approval whose signed `target_seq` IS that step — a\n5\treplay onto a different action, a wrong token, an unsigned/denied approval → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n9\tfrom .approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n10\tfrom .approvals import verify_approval\n11\t\n12\tACTION_SIGNAL = \"web.actor.step\"\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tdef action_token(service: str, step_kind: str, page_sha256: str, field_binding: str) -> str:\n16\t    return sha256_hex(f\"{service}|{step_kind}|{page_sha256}|{field_binding}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef action_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n20\t    rec = store.get(seq)\n21\t    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != ACTION_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"action_token\") != token:\n22\t        return False\n23\t    from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n24\t    devices = authorized_devices(store, trusted_pubkey)\n25\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n26\t        p = r.payload\n27\t        if (p.get(\"signal\") == _APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == seq\n28\t                and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=devices)):\n29\t            return True\n30\t    return False\n31\t"}]},"uuid":"494c3117-e209-4da2-8b9e-4fe919c3e248","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:16.361Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2f104626-edba-4f4f-8a66-d3cb68f1987c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"494c3117-e209-4da2-8b9e-4fe919c3e248","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHM6Ht5kbavcvD248sL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012gtqGjFVX6MJ5inRz68guN","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/actor_scope.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4803,"cache_read_input_tokens":32318,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4803,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHM5Ma9G1mcVRwnjLth","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"5f69d69d-4f4e-490c-8ee4-8403454c09ff","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:16.873Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"5f69d69d-4f4e-490c-8ee4-8403454c09ff","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012gtqGjFVX6MJ5inRz68guN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"ActorScope (Phase 8, WS-G G-iv) — the origin allowlist that bounds where DELEGATE may act (the\n2\towner's OWN service integrations), mirroring `OperatorScope`: EMPTY = deny-all. A step's origin must\n3\tbe (1) http/https, (2) public (`sources.is_public_host` — no internal hosts), (3) in the allowlist.\n4\tPlus a per-service `creation_cap` (default 1) — mass account creation is out of doctrine; the cap is\n5\tchecked at preview AND execute.\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n9\tfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n10\t\n11\tfrom .sources import is_public_host\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tdef _origin(url: str) -> Optional[str]:\n15\t    p = urlsplit(url if \"//\" in url else \"https://\" + url)\n16\t    if p.scheme not in (\"http\", \"https\") or not p.hostname:\n17\t        return None\n18\t    return f\"{p.scheme}://{p.hostname.lower()}\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass ActorScope:\n22\t    def __init__(self, allowed_origins: Optional[List[str]] = None, *, creation_cap: int = 1):\n23\t        self.allowed = {o for o in (_origin(a) for a in (allowed_origins or [])) if o}\n24\t        self.creation_cap = creation_cap\n25\t\n26\t    def origin_allowed(self, url: str) -> bool:\n27\t        p = urlsplit(url)\n28\t        if p.scheme not in (\"http\", \"https\") or not p.hostname or not is_public_host(p.hostname):\n29\t            return False\n30\t        return bool(self.allowed) and f\"{p.scheme}://{p.hostname.lower()}\" in self.allowed\n31\t\n32\t    def creation_allowed(self, store, service: str) -> bool:\n33\t        n = sum(1 for r in store.iter_records()\n34\t                if r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"web.actor.step\"\n35\t                and r.payload.get(\"step_kind\") == \"account.create\"\n36\t                and r.payload.get(\"service\") == service\n37\t                and r.payload.get(\"status\") == \"applied\")\n38\t        return n < self.creation_cap\n39\t"}]},"uuid":"89130fc3-ff0f-4c55-b044-e45ea91df003","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:16.886Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5f69d69d-4f4e-490c-8ee4-8403454c09ff","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"89130fc3-ff0f-4c55-b044-e45ea91df003","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHM6Ht5kbavcvD248sL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EG8Cx5NT1tSETmqTp6RbPu","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/web_engine.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4803,"cache_read_input_tokens":32318,"output_tokens":377,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4803},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":377,"cache_read_input_tokens":32318,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4803,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4803,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHM5Ma9G1mcVRwnjLth","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"34ad546e-c76c-42cf-9de2-f4c300f10e96","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:17.255Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"34ad546e-c76c-42cf-9de2-f4c300f10e96","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EG8Cx5NT1tSETmqTp6RbPu","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Web engine (Phase 8, WS-G G-iii) — HTTP-first, headless-browser fallback. `HttpEngine` reuses the\n2\tSSRF-gated, IP-pinned, correlatable-UA `sources.fetch_raw` for the light path. `detect_block` flags a\n3\tCAPTCHA / 403 / 429 / CF-challenge and is checked BEFORE the escalation decision, so \"use the browser\n4\tto defeat a block\" is structurally unreachable. `BrowserEngine` (lazy Playwright, locked-down, no\n5\tstealth/proxy/UA-rotation) is a documented off-by-default seam. `FakeEngine` is the deterministic\n6\tdouble + call spy for tests.\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\timport re\n10\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n11\tfrom typing import Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n12\t\n13\t_BLOCK = re.compile(r\"recaptcha|hcaptcha|turnstile|cf-chl|just a moment|attention required|\\bcaptcha\\b\", re.I)\n14\t_JS_APP = re.compile(r'id=[\"\\'](root|app|__next)[\"\\']', re.I)\n15\t\n16\t\n17\t@dataclass\n18\tclass PageView:\n19\t    ok: bool\n20\t    status: int\n21\t    html: str\n22\t    url: str\n23\t\n24\t\n25\t@runtime_checkable\n26\tclass WebEngine(Protocol):\n27\t    egresses: bool\n28\t    def fetch(self, url: str) -> PageView: ...\n29\t    def act(self, step: dict, resolved: dict) -> dict: ...\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tdef detect_block(status: int, html: str) -> Optional[str]:\n33\t    \"\"\"A block signal (STOP + surface as a positive control — never defeated). Runs BEFORE escalation.\"\"\"\n34\t    if status in (403, 429):\n35\t        return f\"http-{status}\"\n36\t    if _BLOCK.search(html or \"\"):\n37\t        return \"captcha/anti-automation\"\n38\t    return None\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tdef needs_js(html: str, *, min_text: int = 200) -> bool:\n42\t    \"\"\"A MISSING-CAPABILITY signal (escalate to the browser) — deliberately DISJOINT from a block.\"\"\"\n43\t    from .sources import _strip_html\n44\t    return len(_strip_html(html or \"\").strip()) < min_text and bool(_JS_APP.search(html or \"\"))\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tclass HttpEngine:\n48\t    egresses = True\n49\t\n50\t    def fetch(self, url: str) -> PageView:\n51\t        from .sources import fetch_raw\n52\t        r = fetch_raw(url)\n53\t        return PageView(r.ok, r.status, r.raw, url)\n54\t\n55\t    def act(self, step: dict, resolved: dict) -> dict:\n56\t        \"\"\"A simple static-form submit (urlencoded POST). Credentials arrive in `resolved` at the last\n57\t        instant and go ONLY into the POST body — never logged/returned.\"\"\"\n58\t        import urllib.parse\n59\t        import urllib.request\n60\t        from .sources import UA, _NoRedirect, _PinnedHTTPHandler, _PinnedHTTPSHandler, _vetted_ip\n61\t        from urllib.parse import urlsplit\n62\t        url = step.get(\"url\", \"\")\n63\t        ip = _vetted_ip(urlsplit(url).hostname or \"\")\n64\t        if ip is None:\n65\t            return {\"ok\": False, \"reason\": \"ssrf-refused\"}\n66\t        data = urllib.parse.urlencode(resolved).encode(\"utf-8\")\n67\t        opener = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}), _PinnedHTTPHandler(ip),\n68\t                                             _PinnedHTTPSHandler(ip), _NoRedirect)\n69\t        req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, method=\"POST\",\n70\t                                     headers={\"User-Agent\": UA, \"Content-Type\": \"application/x-www-form-urlencoded\"})\n71\t        try:\n72\t            with opener.open(req, timeout=20) as r:\n73\t                return {\"ok\": 200 <= getattr(r, \"status\", 200) < 400, \"status\": getattr(r, \"status\", 200)}\n74\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n75\t            return {\"ok\": False, \"reason\": f\"{type(e).__name__}\"}\n76\t\n77\t\n78\tclass FakeEngine:\n79\t    \"\"\"Deterministic double + spy: `pages` = {url: (status, html)}; records every fetch/act.\"\"\"\n80\t    egresses = True\n81\t\n82\t    def __init__(self, pages: Optional[dict] = None):\n83\t        self.pages = pages or {}\n84\t        self.calls: list = []\n85\t\n86\t    def fetch(self, url: str) -> PageView:\n87\t        self.calls.append((\"fetch\", url))\n88\t        st, html = self.pages.get(url, (404, \"\"))\n89\t        return PageView(200 <= st < 300, st, html, url)\n90\t\n91\t    def act(self, step: dict, resolved: dict) -> dict:\n92\t        self.calls.append((\"act\", step.get(\"url\"), sorted(resolved.keys())))   # keys only — never values\n93\t        return {\"ok\": True, \"status\": 200}\n94\t"}]},"uuid":"af753744-e0a6-4634-8d86-603d3c16b8e7","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:17.289Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"34ad546e-c76c-42cf-9de2-f4c300f10e96","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"06b76e48-8ac0-42dc-967d-b6cc9f632221","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHMWFmzRbPA3oPMHXJv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the substrate: base, approvals, governor, spine, mesh, operator, sources."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10181,"cache_read_input_tokens":37121,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":10181,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHMUizzsRLvdMMtfCqQ","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"18ea0542-76d4-4991-8e89-ac94d1321afc","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:22.433Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"18ea0542-76d4-4991-8e89-ac94d1321afc","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHMWFmzRbPA3oPMHXJv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012xMQecVfopJ5V5xp1sxDcU","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/base.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10181,"cache_read_input_tokens":37121,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":10181,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHMUizzsRLvdMMtfCqQ","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"e316ee7d-f90e-45ad-8c27-3e323d093a8a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:23.063Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"e316ee7d-f90e-45ad-8c27-3e323d093a8a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012xMQecVfopJ5V5xp1sxDcU","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL agent mesh framework (SIGIL §4/§5). Each agent has a MANDATE and an AUTONOMY\n2\tCEILING (A0–A3); everything it does is a `Proposal` carrying a tier. Proposals at or below the\n3\tauto bar (A1) and at or below the agent's ceiling are AUTO-APPLIED (written to the spine);\n4\tanything higher is QUEUED as a pending record a human must approve — never auto-executed.\n5\t\n6\tThe doctrine is enforced STRUCTURALLY, not by discipline: an agent literally cannot take an\n7\texternal-effect action, because no agent has a send/deploy/spend code path. ENVOY, in\n8\tparticular, drafts only — its ceiling is A2 and there is no method that transmits. Agent output\n9\tis provenance-linked spine records, so a brief or a draft is auditable back to what produced it.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n13\tfrom enum import IntEnum\n14\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n17\t\n18\tAGENTS_SOURCE = \"agent\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass Tier(IntEnum):\n22\t    \"\"\"Mirror of the WARDEN tiers (SIGIL §5), ordered so `<=` compares autonomy.\"\"\"\n23\t    A0 = 0   # observe / answer\n24\t    A1 = 1   # reversible internal act (write a report/brief/event) — the AUTO bar\n25\t    A2 = 2   # external-visible / semi-reversible (send, calendar write) — QUEUED\n26\t    A3 = 3   # destructive / financial / security — explicit, never auto\n27\t\n28\t    def label(self) -> str:\n29\t        return self.name\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tAUTO_BAR = Tier.A1  # A0/A1 auto-apply; A2/A3 queue for human approval\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t@dataclass\n36\tclass Proposal:\n37\t    \"\"\"One thing an agent wants to do: emit a record (auto) or take a gated action (queued).\"\"\"\n38\t    kind: str                       # spine record kind (event/finding/brief/interaction/draft/...)\n39\t    payload: dict\n40\t    tier: Tier = Tier.A1\n41\t    parent_id: Optional[int] = None\n42\t    supersedes_id: Optional[int] = None   # this record supersedes an earlier one (e.g. a resolution)\n43\t\n44\t\n45\t@dataclass\n46\tclass AgentResult:\n47\t    agent: str\n48\t    applied: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)     # seqs written (auto)\n49\t    queued: List[dict] = field(default_factory=list)      # proposals held for approval\n50\t    notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tclass Agent:\n54\t    \"\"\"Base agent. Subclasses implement `run()`; the base routes every Proposal through the GOVERNOR\n55\t    (SIGIL §5: kill switch, budgets, promotion — Phase 6) and records the outcome on the spine.\n56\t    Backward compatible: with no kill/cap/promotion the governor reproduces the original gate.\"\"\"\n57\t    name: str = \"AGENT\"\n58\t    mandate: str = \"\"\n59\t    ceiling: Tier = Tier.A1\n60\t\n61\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, governor=None):\n62\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n63\t        if governor is None:\n64\t            from ..governor import Governor\n65\t            governor = Governor(self.store)\n66\t        self.governor = governor\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self) -> AgentResult:\n69\t        raise NotImplementedError\n70\t\n71\t    def _dispatch(self, proposals: List[Proposal]) -> AgentResult:\n72\t        from ..governor import Outcome\n73\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n74\t        for p in proposals:\n75\t            effective = max(p.tier, Tier.A0)\n76\t            # promotion scope = the RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label:\n77\t            # a promotion is \"this agent may auto-approve A2 records of this kind\" (red-pen RP-3).\n78\t            decision = self.governor.decide(agent=self.name, tier=effective,\n79\t                                            ceiling=self.ceiling, scope=p.kind)\n80\t            common = {**p.payload, \"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n81\t                      \"governor\": decision.reason}\n82\t            if decision.outcome == Outcome.AUTO:\n83\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n84\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n85\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n86\t                res.applied.append(seq)\n87\t            elif decision.outcome == Outcome.QUEUE:\n88\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n89\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"queued\", \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\"},\n90\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n91\t                res.queued.append({\"seq\": seq, \"kind\": p.kind, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n92\t                                   \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\") or p.payload.get(\"to\")})\n93\t            else:  # DENY — kill switch or budget; record the refusal, take no action\n94\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n95\t                                  payload={\"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n96\t                                           \"decision\": \"denied\", \"governor\": decision.reason,\n97\t                                           \"of_kind\": p.kind, \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\")},\n98\t                                  parent_id=p.parent_id)\n99\t                res.notes.append(f\"DENIED {p.kind} ({effective.label()}): {decision.reason}\")\n100\t        return res\n101\t"}]},"uuid":"797bcc0b-1e94-4dc8-ae6d-20f41f577fc9","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:23.083Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e316ee7d-f90e-45ad-8c27-3e323d093a8a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"797bcc0b-1e94-4dc8-ae6d-20f41f577fc9","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHMWFmzRbPA3oPMHXJv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01QNNdwKVBdELBZe112VdxUa","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/approvals.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10181,"cache_read_input_tokens":37121,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":10181,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHMUizzsRLvdMMtfCqQ","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"d0b4ba44-9abb-434b-8e09-93fbdc92eafd","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:23.729Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"d0b4ba44-9abb-434b-8e09-93fbdc92eafd","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QNNdwKVBdELBZe112VdxUa","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str], *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid signature over its (target, decision, approver) by the\n35\t    OWNER key OR by a currently-authorized DEVICE key (`extra_pubkeys`). The claimed `pubkey` must be\n36\t    in the allowed set — which is composed ONLY of owner-minted keys (the owner identity + the\n37\t    owner-signed device-authorization ledger, see `sigil.mesh`) — so a key the requester merely\n38\t    presents is never trusted (RP-APPROVAL-2). The signed `target_seq` still binds (RP-APPROVAL-4).\n39\t    Unsigned / not-in-allowed-set / no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n40\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n41\t    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n42\t    allowed = set()\n43\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n44\t        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n45\t    if extra_pubkeys:\n46\t        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n47\t    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n48\t        return False\n49\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n50\t    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n54\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n55\t    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n56\t    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n57\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n58\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n59\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n60\t    resolved = set()\n61\t    queued = {}\n62\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n63\t        p = r.payload\n64\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64, extra_pubkeys=extra_pubkeys):\n65\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n66\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n67\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n68\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n72\t    pass\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n76\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n77\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n78\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n79\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n81\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n82\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n83\t\n84\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n85\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n86\t            if r.seq == seq:\n87\t                return r\n88\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n89\t\n90\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n91\t        target = self._target(seq)\n92\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n93\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n94\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n95\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n96\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n97\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n98\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n99\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n100\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n101\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n102\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n103\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n104\t\n105\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n106\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n107\t\n108\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n109\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"c9bfde3a-5182-4cda-bb9d-6e28d65ca9a2","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:23.771Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d0b4ba44-9abb-434b-8e09-93fbdc92eafd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"38e01b6f-0dc8-4912-a367-980963622996","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KvfchG7ejm5kRPunN1zrKN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL governor (Phase 6, SIGIL §5 mechanics) — the runtime enforcement of the WARDEN autonomy\n2\tmodel for the AGENT MESH (the Rust `kernel/` WARDEN governs kernel-level actions; this is its\n3\tPython-side mirror for agents). Every agent Proposal is decided here BEFORE it is written:\n4\t\n5\t  • KILL SWITCH (sealed latch): when engaged, the mesh is halted — only A0 observe/read survives.\n6\t  • BUDGETS: per-agent daily action/interrupt caps, fail-closed (over budget → deny + log).\n7\t  • PROMOTION: a per-(agent, scope) grant may auto-approve A2 — EXCEPT ENVOY outbound, which has NO\n8\t    promotion path by construction (SIGIL §4.6). A3 never auto-promotes.\n9\t\n10\tAll state lives on the append-only spine (kill/promotion are events; budget spend is derived from\n11\tthe log), so the governor's every decision is itself auditable — which is what makes the acceptance\n12\tbar (\"zero unauthorized A2/A3 in the audit log\") provable rather than promised. Offense-free by\n13\tdoctrine (assert_no_offense).\"\"\"\n14\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n15\t\n16\tassert_no_offense()\n17\t\n18\tfrom .budget import BudgetCaps, BudgetLedger  # noqa: E402\n19\tfrom .core import Decision, Governor, Outcome  # noqa: E402\n20\tfrom .killswitch import KillSwitch  # noqa: E402\n21\tfrom .promotion import PromotionPolicy  # noqa: E402\n22\t\n23\t__all__ = [\"Governor\", \"Decision\", \"Outcome\", \"KillSwitch\", \"BudgetCaps\", \"BudgetLedger\",\n24\t           \"PromotionPolicy\"]\n25\t"}]},"uuid":"4caef029-7a55-420c-b96b-f9a84772b599","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:23.996Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"38e01b6f-0dc8-4912-a367-980963622996","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"4caef029-7a55-420c-b96b-f9a84772b599","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHMWFmzRbPA3oPMHXJv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Cciv994S7DYrzyDD7QXqj3","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/governor/promotion.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10181,"cache_read_input_tokens":37121,"output_tokens":362,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":10181},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":362,"cache_read_input_tokens":37121,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10181,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":10181,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHMUizzsRLvdMMtfCqQ","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"3775415b-ed49-4456-aacc-779b53a973be","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:24.409Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"3775415b-ed49-4456-aacc-779b53a973be","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Cciv994S7DYrzyDD7QXqj3","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-scope promotion policy (SIGIL §5). An owner may promote a specific (agent, scope) so its A2\n2\tproposals auto-approve instead of queuing — EXCEPT ENVOY, which has NO promotion path (§4.6). A3\n3\tnever promotes.\n4\t\n5\tAUTHENTICATED (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2/RP-4): a grant/revoke is meaningless unless SIGNED by the owner\n6\tkey and verified against the persisted trusted pubkey. `is_promoted` ignores any grant that does not\n7\tverify — so a forged grant written by a prompt-injected agent via `self.store` grants nothing. The\n8\tgate scope is the proposal's RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label\n9\t(RP-3), so a promotion is \"agent X may auto-approve A2 records of kind K.\" \"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom typing import Optional\n13\t\n14\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n15\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n16\t\n17\tSIGNAL = \"governor.promotion\"\n18\tNO_PROMOTION_AGENTS = frozenset({\"ENVOY\", \"DELEGATE\"})   # outbound + account actions stay human-gated forever\n19\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"agent\", \"scope\")\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tclass PromotionPolicy:\n23\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n24\t        self.store = store\n25\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n26\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n27\t\n28\t    def grant(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\"):\n29\t        if agent in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS:\n30\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\",\n31\t                              payload={\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"refused\", \"agent\": agent,\n32\t                                       \"scope\": scope, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n33\t                                       \"reason\": f\"{agent} has no promotion path (SIGIL §4.6) — outbound stays human-gated\"})\n34\t            return None\n35\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"granted\", \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope}\n36\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": \"owner\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n37\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n38\t\n39\t    def revoke(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\") -> int:\n40\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope}\n41\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": \"owner\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n42\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n43\t\n44\t    def is_promoted(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\") -> bool:\n45\t        if agent in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS:\n46\t            return False                          # never — structural, not policy\n47\t        state: dict = {}\n48\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n49\t            p = r.payload\n50\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL or p.get(\"state\") not in (\"granted\", \"revoked\"):\n51\t                continue\n52\t            if not verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n53\t                continue                          # fail-closed: an unsigned/forged grant grants nothing\n54\t            state[(p.get(\"agent\"), p.get(\"scope\"))] = p[\"state\"]   # latest verified wins\n55\t        return state.get((agent, scope)) == \"granted\" or state.get((agent, \"*\")) == \"granted\"\n56\t"}]},"uuid":"43a28414-b668-47d8-ae31-36787a6ad034","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:24.447Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3775415b-ed49-4456-aacc-779b53a973be","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
