{"parentUuid":"8f55c11f-9ae3-4ade-86c2-22cdaa7ccba5","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHN3ykSB1HzWVXss3NN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the governor core, spine store, mesh, sources, and operator."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7370,"cache_read_input_tokens":47302,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":7370,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHN2vFkAxqhEGqXWyEg","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"182b0f15-2b04-4a6c-baea-beef8cec3750","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:29.656Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"182b0f15-2b04-4a6c-baea-beef8cec3750","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHN3ykSB1HzWVXss3NN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017k8MwENMz4yqh1kmzLQgUj","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/governor/core.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7370,"cache_read_input_tokens":47302,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":7370,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHN2vFkAxqhEGqXWyEg","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"984f1ecc-a778-4357-b58c-081873a328d6","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:30.325Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"984f1ecc-a778-4357-b58c-081873a328d6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017k8MwENMz4yqh1kmzLQgUj","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The Governor (SIGIL §5) — the single decision point the agent dispatch gate consults for EVERY\n2\tproposal. It composes the kill switch, budgets, and promotion policy into one fail-closed verdict:\n3\tAUTO (write it), QUEUE (hold for human approval), or DENY (refuse + log). The order matters and is\n4\tdeliberately conservative: observe is always allowed; a kill halts everything above observe; budget\n5\tis checked before any auto-grant; A2 auto-approves only under an explicit promotion; A3 never autos.\n6\t\n7\tKill/promotion are read FRESH per decision (a mid-run kill halts the rest of the run). Backward\n8\tcompatible: with no kill, no cap, and no promotion, A0/A1≤ceiling → AUTO and A2/A3 → QUEUE, exactly\n9\tas the pre-Phase-6 gate behaved.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n13\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n14\tfrom enum import Enum\n15\tfrom typing import Optional\n16\t\n17\tfrom .budget import BudgetCaps, BudgetLedger\n18\tfrom .killswitch import KillSwitch\n19\tfrom .promotion import PromotionPolicy\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tclass Outcome(Enum):\n23\t    AUTO = \"auto\"\n24\t    QUEUE = \"queue\"\n25\t    DENY = \"deny\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n29\tclass Decision:\n30\t    outcome: Outcome\n31\t    reason: str\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tdef _utc_day() -> str:\n35\t    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()   # runtime bucketing only (never digested)\n36\t\n37\t\n38\tdef _load_caps() -> BudgetCaps:\n39\t    \"\"\"Optional per-agent caps from ~/.sigil/budgets.json ({daily_actions, daily_interrupts}).\n40\t    Absent/malformed → uncapped (budgets opt-in; default behavior unchanged).\"\"\"\n41\t    try:\n42\t        import json\n43\t\n44\t        from ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n45\t        f = SIGIL_HOME / \"budgets.json\"\n46\t        if not f.exists():\n47\t            return BudgetCaps()\n48\t        d = json.loads(f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n49\t        return BudgetCaps(daily_actions=d.get(\"daily_actions\"), daily_interrupts=d.get(\"daily_interrupts\"))\n50\t    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):\n51\t        return BudgetCaps()\n52\t\n53\t\n54\tclass Governor:\n55\t    def __init__(self, store, *, caps: Optional[BudgetCaps] = None, day_iso: Optional[str] = None,\n56\t                 owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n57\t        from .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n58\t        self.store = store\n59\t        ok = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n60\t        tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n61\t        self.kill = KillSwitch(store, owner_key=ok, trusted_pubkey=tp)\n62\t        self.promo = PromotionPolicy(store, owner_key=ok, trusted_pubkey=tp)\n63\t        self.budget = BudgetLedger(store, caps if caps is not None else _load_caps())\n64\t        self._day = day_iso\n65\t\n66\t    def decide(self, *, agent: str, tier, ceiling, scope: Optional[str] = None) -> Decision:\n67\t        from ..agents.base import AUTO_BAR, Tier\n68\t        scope = scope or \"*\"\n69\t\n70\t        # A0 observe/read is ALWAYS allowed — even under a kill switch (perception + memory stay alive).\n71\t        if tier <= Tier.A0:\n72\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, \"observe (A0)\")\n73\t\n74\t        # KILL SWITCH: the mesh is halted; nothing above observe may act.\n75\t        if self.kill.is_engaged():\n76\t            return Decision(Outcome.DENY, \"kill switch engaged — agent mesh halted (A0 observe only)\")\n77\t\n78\t        # BUDGET: fail-closed — at cap, deny.\n79\t        over, why = self.budget.over_budget(agent, self._day or _utc_day())\n80\t        if over:\n81\t            return Decision(Outcome.DENY, why)\n82\t\n83\t        # Within the auto bar (A0/A1) and the agent's ceiling → auto, as always.\n84\t        if tier <= AUTO_BAR and tier <= ceiling:\n85\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, f\"within auto bar ({tier.label()})\")\n86\t\n87\t        # A2 may auto-approve ONLY under an explicit per-scope promotion (never for ENVOY).\n88\t        if tier == Tier.A2 and tier <= ceiling and self.promo.is_promoted(agent, scope):\n89\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, f\"A2 auto-approved by promotion ({agent}/{scope})\")\n90\t\n91\t        # Everything else (A2 unpromoted, A3, or above ceiling) queues for explicit human approval.\n92\t        return Decision(Outcome.QUEUE, f\"queued for approval ({tier.label()})\")\n93\t"}]},"uuid":"a4e4944d-319d-4095-b805-d9810319a8fc","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:30.355Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"984f1ecc-a778-4357-b58c-081873a328d6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"5b2057be-35bb-4cff-9108-adb06f3e059f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VUSpW6GMiLakT42s3eUXQG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Append-only, hash-chained JSONL spine (SIGIL §6.1, D1).\n2\t\n3\tReuses CRUCIBLE's tamper-evident chain verbatim (`sigil.reuse`): each line carries\n4\t`{seq, prev_hash, entry_hash}` where `entry_hash` links prev+cert_digest+seq. The\n5\t`cert_digest` is over the record's CONTENT only (scope/kind/source/actor/payload/\n6\tparent/supersedes) — NOT the wallclock `ts` — so the chain is replay-stable. Appends\n7\tare O(1): read the last line's entry, `append_entry`, write.\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport json\n12\timport os\n13\tfrom pathlib import Path\n14\tfrom typing import Any, Iterator\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..config import SCOPE, SPINE_PATH\n17\tfrom ..reuse import ChainEntry, append_entry, build_chain, digest_payload, verify_chain\n18\tfrom .models import SpineRecord, now_iso\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef _last_nonempty_line(path: Path) -> str | None:\n22\t    \"\"\"Read the last non-empty line without loading the file (seek-from-end).\"\"\"\n23\t    with path.open(\"rb\") as f:\n24\t        f.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)\n25\t        end = f.tell()\n26\t        if end == 0:\n27\t            return None\n28\t        buf = b\"\"\n29\t        pos = end\n30\t        while pos > 0:\n31\t            step = min(8192, pos)\n32\t            pos -= step\n33\t            f.seek(pos)\n34\t            buf = f.read(step) + buf\n35\t            lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n36\t            if lines and (pos == 0 or buf.count(b\"\\n\") >= 2):\n37\t                return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\")\n38\t        lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n39\t        return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\") if lines else None\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tclass SpineStore:\n43\t    def __init__(self, path: Path | str = SPINE_PATH) -> None:\n44\t        self.path = Path(path)\n45\t        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n46\t        self._last: ChainEntry | None = self._read_last_entry()\n47\t\n48\t    # --- write --------------------------------------------------------------------\n49\t    def append(\n50\t        self, *, kind: str, source: str, actor: str, payload: dict[str, Any],\n51\t        parent_id: int | None = None, supersedes_id: int | None = None,\n52\t        ts: str | None = None,\n53\t    ) -> int:\n54\t        content = {\n55\t            \"scope\": SCOPE, \"kind\": kind, \"source\": source, \"actor\": actor,\n56\t            \"payload\": payload, \"parent_id\": parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": supersedes_id,\n57\t        }\n58\t        cert_digest = digest_payload(content)  # wallclock-free\n59\t        entry = append_entry([self._last], cert_digest) if self._last else build_chain([cert_digest])[0]\n60\t        record = {\n61\t            \"seq\": entry.seq, **content, \"ts\": ts or now_iso(),\n62\t            \"cert_digest\": cert_digest, \"prev_hash\": entry.prev_hash, \"entry_hash\": entry.entry_hash,\n63\t        }\n64\t        with self.path.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n65\t            f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n66\t        self._last = entry\n67\t        return entry.seq\n68\t\n69\t    # --- read ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n70\t    def iter_records(self, *, since_seq: int = -1) -> Iterator[SpineRecord]:\n71\t        if not self.path.exists():\n72\t            return\n73\t        with self.path.open(encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n74\t            for line in f:\n75\t                line = line.strip()\n76\t                if not line:\n77\t                    continue\n78\t                d = json.loads(line)\n79\t                if d[\"seq\"] > since_seq:\n80\t                    yield SpineRecord.from_dict(d)\n81\t\n82\t    def get(self, seq: int) -> SpineRecord | None:\n83\t        for r in self.iter_records(since_seq=seq - 1):\n84\t            if r.seq == seq:\n85\t                return r\n86\t            if r.seq > seq:\n87\t                break\n88\t        return None\n89\t\n90\t    @property\n91\t    def next_seq(self) -> int:\n92\t        return (self._last.seq + 1) if self._last else 0\n93\t\n94\t    def count(self) -> int:\n95\t        return sum(1 for _ in self.iter_records())\n96\t\n97\t    # --- integrity ----------------------------------------------------------------\n98\t    def entries(self) -> list[ChainEntry]:\n99\t        return [\n100\t            ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash)\n101\t            for r in self.iter_records()\n102\t        ]\n103\t\n104\t    def verify(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n105\t        \"\"\"Two-layer UNKEYED integrity: (1) BINDING — each record's payload still hashes to its\n106\t        stored cert_digest (catches silent payload edits); (2) CHAIN — the entries link cleanly\n107\t        (catches delete/reorder/entry tamper). This proves internal CONSISTENCY, not authenticity:\n108\t        a naive payload edit fails (1), and a mid-chain digest edit cascades an entry_hash/prev_hash\n109\t        break caught by (2) — BUT a writer who recomputes cert_digest+entry_hash for the tip (no\n110\t        successor to cascade into) or forward-cascades a fork produces a self-consistent chain that\n111\t        passes here. Resistance to a recompute-capable writer is the owner-SIGNED head's job\n112\t        (`checkpoint.verify_checkpoint`, Ed25519 + monotonic last_seq). Use this for corruption/\n113\t        naive-tamper detection; use the signed head for tamper-EVIDENCE.\"\"\"\n114\t        entries: list[ChainEntry] = []\n115\t        for r in self.iter_records():\n116\t            content = {\n117\t                \"scope\": r.scope, \"kind\": r.kind, \"source\": r.source, \"actor\": r.actor,\n118\t                \"payload\": r.payload, \"parent_id\": r.parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": r.supersedes_id,\n119\t            }\n120\t            if digest_payload(content) != r.cert_digest:\n121\t                return False, f\"binding break at seq {r.seq}: payload does not match cert_digest (record tampered)\"\n122\t            entries.append(ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash))\n123\t        return verify_chain(entries)\n124\t\n125\t    def _read_last_entry(self) -> ChainEntry | None:\n126\t        line = _last_nonempty_line(self.path) if self.path.exists() else None\n127\t        if not line:\n128\t            return None\n129\t        d = json.loads(line)\n130\t        return ChainEntry(seq=d[\"seq\"], prev_hash=d[\"prev_hash\"], cert_digest=d[\"cert_digest\"], entry_hash=d[\"entry_hash\"])\n131\t"}]},"uuid":"275df795-77ae-431e-9983-fc4fc110ed95","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:30.680Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5b2057be-35bb-4cff-9108-adb06f3e059f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"2cfbc1d0-90ea-46d5-b957-52d5b0c1e7a2","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FqSiJWrHrqYtQmSpZEbRSk","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The mesh registry (Phase 7, WS-D). Two owner-signed ledgers on the one spine:\n2\t\n3\t  • host_capability — each host advertises {os, screen, camera, gpu_vlm, always_on}, owner-signed, so\n4\t    an agent can route work to the best-capable ONLINE host from cited, tamper-evident records.\n5\t  • device authorization — a 1-of-N device ledger: the owner AUTHORIZES a phone's own Ed25519 device\n6\t    key with a signed `device_authorized` record (and `device_revoked` to remove it). The phone then\n7\t    approves A2/A3 items by signing with ITS key; `verify_approval(..., extra_pubkeys=authorized)`\n8\t    accepts it. Trust is pinned to owner-MINTED keys (RP-APPROVAL-2); the phone never holds the\n9\t    trust-root key, and can approve offline.\n10\t\n11\tAll reads are fail-closed: a descriptor/authorization that does not verify against the owner key is\n12\tignored. Reuses `governor.authn` (the same signed-event primitive kill/promotion/approval use).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Set\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import _approval_message\n19\tfrom ..governor.authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n20\tfrom ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n21\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex, sign\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tCAP_SIGNAL = \"mesh.host_capability\"\n25\tDEV_SIGNAL = \"mesh.device\"\n26\t_CAP_CORE = (\"signal\", \"host_id\", \"os\", \"has_screen\", \"has_camera\", \"has_gpu_vlm\", \"always_on\",\n27\t             \"has_hid_inject\", \"has_camera_stream\")\n28\t_DEV_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"device_id\", \"device_pubkey\")\n29\t\n30\t\n31\t# --- host capability advertisement -----------------------------------------------------------------\n32\tdef advertise_capability(store: SpineStore, descriptor: dict, owner_key) -> int:\n33\t    core = {\"signal\": CAP_SIGNAL, \"host_id\": descriptor[\"host_id\"], \"os\": descriptor[\"os\"],\n34\t            \"has_screen\": bool(descriptor[\"has_screen\"]), \"has_camera\": bool(descriptor[\"has_camera\"]),\n35\t            \"has_gpu_vlm\": bool(descriptor[\"has_gpu_vlm\"]), \"always_on\": bool(descriptor[\"always_on\"]),\n36\t            \"has_hid_inject\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_hid_inject\", False)),\n37\t            \"has_camera_stream\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_camera_stream\", False))}\n38\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n39\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef capability_map(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n43\t    \"\"\"Latest VERIFIED capability per host_id (forged/unsigned advertisements ignored).\"\"\"\n44\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n45\t    latest: dict = {}\n46\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n47\t        p = r.payload\n48\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == CAP_SIGNAL and verify_signed(p, _CAP_CORE, tp):\n49\t            latest[p.get(\"host_id\")] = {k: p.get(k) for k in _CAP_CORE if k != \"signal\"}\n50\t    return latest\n51\t\n52\t\n53\t# --- device authorization ledger -------------------------------------------------------------------\n54\tdef authorize_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n55\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"authorized\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n56\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n57\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef revoke_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n61\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n62\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n63\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef authorized_devices(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> Set[str]:\n67\t    \"\"\"The set of currently-authorized device pubkeys (owner-signed authorize, minus later revoke).\n68\t    An unsigned/forged authorization is ignored — a rogue device cannot self-authorize.\"\"\"\n69\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n70\t    state: dict = {}\n71\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n72\t        p = r.payload\n73\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == DEV_SIGNAL and p.get(\"state\") in (\"authorized\", \"revoked\") \\\n74\t                and verify_signed(p, _DEV_CORE, tp):\n75\t            state[p.get(\"device_pubkey\")] = p[\"state\"]      # latest verified state per device key\n76\t    return {pub for pub, st in state.items() if st == \"authorized\"}\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tclass DeviceApprover:\n80\t    \"\"\"Signs an approval with a DEVICE key (the phone). The record verifies via\n81\t    `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(store))` — i.e. only while\n82\t    the device remains authorized. Never touches the owner trust-root key.\"\"\"\n83\t    def __init__(self, store: SpineStore, *, device_key):\n84\t        self.store = store\n85\t        self.device_key = device_key\n86\t\n87\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, approver: str, reason: str) -> int:\n88\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n89\t        sig = sign(self.device_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n90\t        payload = {\"signal\": _APPROVAL_SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq,\n91\t                   \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason, \"pubkey\": self.device_key.public_key_b64,\n92\t                   \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg), \"device\": True, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n93\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n94\t\n95\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver, reason)\n97\t\n98\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n99\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver, reason)\n100\t"}]},"uuid":"04d9a402-888d-491b-9298-a651d92fc114","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:38.131Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2cfbc1d0-90ea-46d5-b957-52d5b0c1e7a2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

IPv4-mapped IPv6); and the connection is then PINNED to that exact\n7\tvetted IP, so a rebinding DNS that flips to an internal address between the check and the fetch cannot\n8\tsteer SCHOLAR onto `169.254.169.254`/`localhost`/an internal service. Redirects are not followed. A\n9\trefused/unreadable source is simply empty evidence, never a fabricated one.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport http.client\n13\timport ipaddress\n14\timport re\n15\timport socket\n16\timport urllib.error\n17\timport urllib.request\n18\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n19\tfrom pathlib import Path\n20\tfrom typing import Optional\n21\tfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n22\t\n23\t# One stable, CORRELATABLE User-Agent (OBSIDIAN OPSEC §VI: identifiable, NOT evasive — never rotated).\n24\tUA = \"SIGIL-SCHOLAR/1.0 (authorized owner research)\"\n25\t\n26\t_TAG = re.compile(r\"<[^>]+>\")\n27\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n28\t_SCRIPT = re.compile(r\"<(script|style)[^>]*>.*?</\\1>\", re.S | re.I)\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tdef _strip_html(html: str) -> str:\n32\t    html = _SCRIPT.sub(\" \", html)\n33\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", _TAG.sub(\" \", html)).strip()\n34\t\n35\t\n36\tdef _vetted_ip(host: str) -> Optional[str]:\n37\t    \"\"\"Resolve `host`; return ONE address to pin iff EVERY resolved address is public unicast, else\n38\t    None. Fail-closed: an unresolvable host, or any private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n39\t    unspecified/IPv4-mapped-internal address, yields None.\"\"\"\n40\t    if not host:\n41\t        return None\n42\t    try:\n43\t        infos = socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)\n44\t    except (socket.gaierror, UnicodeError, OSError):\n45\t        return None\n46\t    if not infos:\n47\t        return None\n48\t    chosen = None\n49\t    for family, _t, _p, _c, sockaddr in infos:\n50\t        try:\n51\t            ip = ipaddress.ip_address(sockaddr[0])\n52\t        except ValueError:\n53\t            return None\n54\t        # unwrap IPv4-mapped/compat IPv6 (::ffff:127.0.0.1) so an internal v4 can't hide as v6\n55\t        if isinstance(ip, ipaddress.IPv6Address) and ip.ipv4_mapped is not None:\n56\t            ip = ip.ipv4_mapped\n57\t        # Reject anything NOT globally routable — `not is_global` also closes RFC 6598 CGNAT/shared\n58\t        # space 100.64.0.0/10 (incl. the Alibaba metadata IP 100.100.100.200) and other non-global\n59\t        # ranges the explicit list misses (red-pen BLOCK-1). The explicit checks stay as defense-in-depth.\n60\t        if (ip.is_private or ip.is_loopback or ip.is_link_local or ip.is_reserved\n61\t                or ip.is_multicast or ip.is_unspecified or not ip.is_global):\n62\t            return None\n63\t        if chosen is None:\n64\t            chosen = sockaddr[0]\n65\t    return chosen\n66\t\n67\t\n68\tdef is_public_host(host: str) -> bool:\n69\t    return _vetted_ip(host) is not None\n70\t\n71\t\n72\tclass _PinnedHTTPConnection(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n73\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n74\t\n75\t    def connect(self):\n76\t        self.sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n77\t                                              self.timeout, self.source_address)\n78\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n79\t            self._tunnel()\n80\t\n81\t\n82\tclass _PinnedHTTPSConnection(http.client.HTTPSConnection):\n83\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n84\t\n85\t    def connect(self):\n86\t        sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n87\t                                        self.timeout, self.source_address)\n88\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n89\t            self.sock = sock\n90\t            self._tunnel()\n91\t            sock = self.sock\n92\t        # SNI + certificate validation still use the ORIGINAL hostname, not the pinned IP.\n93\t        self.sock = self._context.wrap_socket(sock, server_hostname=self.host)\n94\t\n95\t\n96\tclass _PinnedHTTPHandler(urllib.request.HTTPHandler):\n97\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n98\t        super().__init__()\n99\t        self._ip = ip\n100\t\n101\t    def http_open(self, req):\n102\t        ip = self._ip\n103\t\n104\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n105\t            c = _PinnedHTTPConnection(host, **kw)\n106\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n107\t            return c\n108\t        return self.do_open(factory, req)\n109\t\n110\t\n111\tclass _PinnedHTTPSHandler(urllib.request.HTTPSHandler):\n112\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n113\t        super().__init__()\n114\t        self._ip = ip\n115\t\n116\t    def https_open(self, req):\n117\t        ip = self._ip\n118\t\n119\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n120\t            c = _PinnedHTTPSConnection(host, **kw)\n121\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n122\t            return c\n123\t        return self.do_open(factory, req, context=self._context)\n124\t\n125\t\n126\tclass _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):\n127\t    \"\"\"Refuse to follow 3xx — a public URL must not redirect SCHOLAR onto an internal host.\"\"\"\n128\t    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):\n129\t        return None\n130\t\n131\t\n132\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n133\tclass FetchResult:\n134\t    \"\"\"The outcome of a raw SSRF-gated fetch. `raw` preserves newlines (robots/extraction need line\n135\t    structure); `status` is surfaced (unlike `read_source`, which swallows to '') so a caller can do\n136\t    robots/backoff on 429/503. `status == 0` = network error / SSRF-refused (nothing left the box).\"\"\"\n137\t    ok: bool\n138\t    status: int\n139\t    raw: str\n140\t    url: str\n141\t    headers: dict = field(default_factory=dict)\n142\t    reason: str = \"\"\n143\t\n144\t\n145\tdef fetch_raw(ref: str, *, timeout: int = 20, max_bytes: int = 2_000_000) -> FetchResult:\n146\t    \"\"\"SSRF-gated raw GET (Phase 8 shared): resolve-once, verify-all-public, PIN the vetted IP, refuse\n147\t    redirects, correlatable UA. Surfaces the HTTP status and preserves the raw body. Never raises. A\n148\t    non-public/unresolvable host → ok=False, status=0 (no request leaves the machine).\"\"\"\n149\t    if not ref.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n150\t        return FetchResult(False, 0, \"\", ref, reason=\"not-http\")\n151\t    host = urlsplit(ref).hostname or \"\"\n152\t    ip = _vetted_ip(host)\n153\t    if ip is None:\n154\t        return FetchResult(False, 0, \"\", ref, reason=\"ssrf-refused\")\n155\t    # ProxyHandler({}) disables env http_proxy/https_proxy so routing can't be diverted (N3); the\n156\t    # pinned handlers force the socket to the vetted IP regardless.\n157\t    opener = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}),\n158\t                                         _PinnedHTTPHandler(ip), _PinnedHTTPSHandler(ip), _NoRedirect)\n159\t    req = urllib.request.Request(ref, headers={\"User-Agent\": UA})\n160\t    try:\n161\t        with opener.open(req, timeout=timeout) as r:\n162\t            raw = r.read(max_bytes).decode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")\n163\t            return FetchResult(True, getattr(r, \"status\", 200), raw, ref, headers=dict(r.headers))\n164\t    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:                 # 3xx-not-followed / 4xx / 5xx — surface the code\n165\t        body = \"\"\n166\t        try:\n167\t            body = e.read(max_bytes).decode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")\n168\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n169\t            pass\n170\t        return FetchResult(False, e.code, body, ref, headers=dict(e.headers or {}), reason=f\"http-{e.code}\")\n171\t    except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError) as e:\n172\t        return FetchResult(False, 0, \"\", ref, reason=f\"neterr:{type(e).__name__}\")\n173\t\n174\t\n175\tdef read_source(ref: str, *, timeout: int = 20, max_chars: int = 20000) -> str:\n176\t    \"\"\"Return the text of a source. `http(s)://…` is SSRF-gated (via `fetch_raw`) then HTML-stripped;\n177\t    anything else is a local path. Never raises — returns '' on failure or refusal.\"\"\"\n178\t    if ref.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n179\t        res = fetch_raw(ref, timeout=timeout)\n180\t        return _strip_html(res.raw)[:max_chars] if res.ok else \"\"\n181\t    try:\n182\t        return Path(ref).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\")[:max_chars]\n183\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — an unreadable source is simply empty evidence\n184\t        return \"\"\n185\t"}]},"uuid":"16b903e8-3985-4dbf-b056-90bd7ce8917a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:39.159Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"896495b1-59dc-4095-b5e5-ccaf85c2fe2a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Ceiling A2 (proposes; destructive steps queue).\n3\t\n4\tPLAN → PREVIEW → APPROVE → EXECUTE → VERIFY → ROLLBACK/UNDO:\n5\t  • tier is DERIVED, never declared — each step's tool token (`fs.read`/`fs.write`/`fs.delete`/\n6\t    `shell.exec.<argv0>`) is classified by the fail-closed Rust oracle (`KernelClassifier`), and the\n7\t    approval requirement is RE-DERIVED from the hash-bound preview at execute (never a trusted field).\n8\t  • scope-gated (two rings): reads/writes must resolve inside the READ ring; a write auto-applies (A1)\n9\t    only inside the narrower AUTO-WRITE ring, else it is A2 (queued). Empty rings = deny-all.\n10\t  • PREVIEW mutates NOTHING; the approval BINDS to the previewed content hash — before executing, the\n11\t    Operator re-previews the CURRENT files and aborts on any mismatch (anti-TOCTOU).\n12\t  • the executable plan (with file contents) lives in a 0700 journal, NOT the append-only spine — no\n13\t    file bytes leak into immutable memory; the spine record carries only hashes.\n14\t  • EXECUTE journals a pre-image (bytes + mode) before each mutating step, applies atomically,\n15\t    RESTORES the original mode, and VERIFIES by re-reading the post-image hash.\n16\t  • ROLLBACK restores every applied step in reverse and is HONEST about a restore that failed or an\n17\t    irreversible `shell` that already ran. UNDO is hash-bound (won't clobber newer work), scope-re-\n18\t    resolved, and single-shot (a second undo is refused).\"\"\"\n19\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n20\t\n21\timport difflib\n22\timport json\n23\timport os\n24\timport shutil\n25\timport subprocess\n26\timport tempfile\n27\tfrom dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field\n28\tfrom pathlib import Path\n29\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n30\t\n31\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n32\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex\n33\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n34\tfrom .operator_scope import OperatorScope\n35\t\n36\t_OP_HOME = SIGIL_HOME / \"operator\"\n37\t_JOURNAL = _OP_HOME / \"journal\"          # per-execute pre-images\n38\t_PLANS = _OP_HOME / \"plans\"              # executable plans (with content) — off the spine\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tdef _hash_bytes(b: bytes) -> str:\n42\t    return sha256_hex(b)\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tdef _secure(d: Path) -> None:\n46\t    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n47\t    try:\n48\t        os.chmod(d, 0o700)\n49\t    except OSError:\n50\t        pass\n51\t\n52\t\n53\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n54\tclass Step:\n55\t    op: str                                   # read | write | delete | shell\n56\t    path: Optional[str] = None\n57\t    content: Optional[str] = None\n58\t    argv: Optional[List[str]] = None\n59\t    cwd: Optional[str] = None\n60\t\n61\t\n62\tdef _tool_token(step: Step) -> str:\n63\t    if step.op in (\"read\", \"write\", \"delete\"):\n64\t        return f\"fs.{step.op}\"\n65\t    if step.op == \"shell\":\n66\t        return f\"shell.exec.{Path((step.argv or ['?'])[0]).name}\"   # 'exec' → A3 by the oracle\n67\t    return \"unknown.op\"                                              # → fail-closed A3\n68\t\n69\t\n70\t@dataclass\n71\tclass StepPreview:\n72\t    step: dict\n73\t    tool_token: str\n74\t    tier: str\n75\t    in_scope: bool\n76\t    reversible: bool\n77\t    detail: str                                # diff/manifest/argv — DISPLAY ONLY (never persisted)\n78\t    pre_hash: Optional[str] = None\n79\t    post_hash: Optional[str] = None\n80\t\n81\t\n82\t@dataclass\n83\tclass PreviewReport:\n84\t    valid: bool\n85\t    plan_hash: str\n86\t    plan_tier: str\n87\t    steps: List[StepPreview] = field(default_factory=list)\n88\t    reason: str = \"\"\n89\t    plan_seq: Optional[int] = None\n90\t\n91\t\n92\tclass Operator(Agent):\n93\t    name = \"OPERATOR\"\n94\t    mandate = \"open files + run commands on request; tiered, previewed, transactional, reversible\"\n95\t    ceiling = Tier.A2\n96\t\n97\t    def __init__(self, store=None, *, scope: Optional[OperatorScope] = None, classifier=None,\n98\t                 trusted_pubkey=None):\n99\t        super().__init__(store)\n100\t        self.scope = scope or OperatorScope()\n101\t        self._classifier = classifier\n102\t        self._trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey\n103\t\n104\t    def _cls(self):\n105\t        if self._classifier is None:\n106\t            from .kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n107\t            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n108\t        return self._classifier\n109\t\n110\t    def _tp(self):\n111\t        if self._trusted_pubkey is None:\n112\t            from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n113\t            self._trusted_pubkey = owner_pubkey()\n114\t        return self._trusted_pubkey\n115\t\n116\t    # --- B3: grounded read (hardlink-guarded) -----------------------------------------------------\n117\t    def read(self, path: str, question: str = \"\") -> AgentResult:\n118\t        rp = self.scope.resolve(path, \"read\")\n119\t        # BLOCK-1: a regular file with >1 hardlink cannot be confined to the ring by path (a link\n120\t        # inside the root may alias an out-of-scope inode) → refuse, fail-closed.\n121\t        if rp is not None and rp.is_file() and rp.stat().st_nlink > 1:\n122\t            rp = None\n123\t        if rp is None:\n124\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n125\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"requested\": path,\n126\t                                       \"reason\": \"path is outside the Operator read scope (or a hardlink)\"})\n127\t            res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n128\t            res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED read {path}: outside scope / hardlink (logged, not read)\")\n129\t            return res\n130\t        try:\n131\t            data = rp.read_bytes()\n132\t        except OSError as e:\n133\t            res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n134\t            res.notes.append(f\"could not read {rp}: {e}\")\n135\t            return res\n136\t        return self._dispatch([Proposal(\"event\", {\n137\t            \"signal\": \"operator.read\", \"subject\": question or str(rp),\n138\t            \"path\": str(rp), \"sha256\": _hash_bytes(data), \"bytes\": len(data),\n139\t            \"summary\": f\"read {rp} ({len(data)} bytes)\",\n140\t            \"content\": data.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\")[:20000],   # verbatim bytes — authoritative\n141\t            \"grounded\": True,\n142\t        }, Tier.A0)])\n143\t\n144\t    # --- B4: preview (no mutation; content journaled off-spine) -----------------------------------\n145\t    def _preview_step(self, step: Step) -> StepPreview:\n146\t        token = _tool_token(step)\n147\t        tier = self._cls().classify(token)\n148\t        in_scope, reversible, detail = True, False, \"\"\n149\t        pre_hash = post_hash = None\n150\t        if step.op == \"shell\":\n151\t            in_scope = self.scope.in_read(step.cwd) if step.cwd else True\n152\t            detail = \"argv: \" + \" \".join(step.argv or []) + (f\"  (cwd {step.cwd})\" if step.cwd else \"\")\n153\t        else:\n154\t            rp = self.scope.resolve(step.path or \"\", \"read\")   # both reads AND writes must be in the READ ring\n155\t            in_scope = rp is not None\n156\t            old = None\n157\t            if rp is not None and rp.exists():\n158\t                try:\n159\t                    old = rp.read_bytes(); pre_hash = _hash_bytes(old)\n160\t                except The trust anchor is the SAME solo-owner\n2\tEd25519 keypair the spine checkpoint already persists (`KEYS_DIR/owner.priv|pub`, the 1-of-1 trust\n3\troot) — governance events (promotion grants, kill release, approvals) are signed by it and verified\n4\tagainst it. Reads are fail-closed: a missing key returns None, so nothing forged is ever trusted.\n5\t\n6\t`owner_pubkey()`/`owner_keypair()` READ the anchor (never generate — verification must not mint\n7\ttrust). `ensure_owner_keypair()` is for owner-initiated SIGNING actions (CLI engage/grant/approve):\n8\tit generates+persists the anchor once if absent, mirroring the checkpoint's `_owner_keys()`.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport os\n12\tfrom typing import Optional\n13\t\n14\tfrom ..config import KEYS_DIR\n15\tfrom ..reuse import KeyPair, generate_keypair\n16\t\n17\t_PRIV = KEYS_DIR / \"owner.priv\"\n18\t_PUB = KEYS_DIR / \"owner.pub\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef owner_pubkey() -> Optional[str]:\n22\t    try:\n23\t        return (_PUB.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip() or None)\n24\t    except OSError:\n25\t        return None\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef owner_keypair() -> Optional[KeyPair]:\n29\t    try:\n30\t        priv = _PRIV.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n31\t        pub = _PUB.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n32\t    except OSError:\n33\t        return None\n34\t    return KeyPair(public_key_b64=pub, private_key_b64=priv) if priv and pub else None\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef ensure_owner_keypair() -> KeyPair:\n38\t    \"\"\"Return the persisted owner keypair, generating+persisting it once if absent (owner signing\n39\t    path only). Same key material the checkpoint uses, so governance and the spine head share one\n40\t    owner identity.\"\"\"\n41\t    kp = owner_keypair()\n42\t    if kp is not None:\n43\t        return kp\n44\t    KEYS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n45\t    kp = generate_keypair()\n46\t    _PRIV.write_text(kp.private_key_b64, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n47\t    _PUB.write_text(kp.public_key_b64, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n48\t    for f in (_PRIV,):\n49\t        try:\n50\t            os.chmod(f, 0o600)\n51\t        except OSError:\n52\t            pass\n53\t    return kp\n54\t"}]},"uuid":"36a06a18-ccda-4c3e-bb03-e1023ab085cc","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:48.470Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2de41f04-18f9-4689-ab65-b8a2529f5379","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"374208fa-2854-4152-a1d3-bba5304d7973","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018uj4qKp2jL7ypetJ4GDTit","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Shared authentication for governance events (Phase 6). Every state-changing governance event\n2\t(promotion grant/revoke, kill engage/release, approval approve/deny) carries an Ed25519 signature by\n3\tthe OWNER key over its canonical authenticated CORE fields, plus the signer's pubkey. Verification is\n4\tfail-closed: no trusted key, no signature, a pubkey that isn't the trusted owner key, or a tampered\n5\tcore → NOT authentic. This is the primitive that makes \"zero unauthorized A2/A3\" provable from the\n6\tlog rather than promised (a forged governance event never verifies, so it is ignored).\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\tfrom typing import Optional, Sequence\n10\t\n11\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sign, verify_one\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tdef _canon(core: dict) -> bytes:\n15\t    m = canonical_json(core)\n16\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef signed_payload(core: dict, owner_key) -> dict:\n20\t    \"\"\"core = the authenticated fields (must be JSON-canonicalizable). Returns core + {sig, pubkey}.\n21\t    With no owner key, sig/pubkey are None → the event will never verify (a caller that requires\n22\t    authentication must reject it).\"\"\"\n23\t    sig = sign(owner_key.private_key_b64, _canon(core)) if owner_key else None\n24\t    return {**core, \"sig\": sig, \"pubkey\": owner_key.public_key_b64 if owner_key else None}\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef verify_signed(payload: dict, core_fields: Sequence[str], trusted_pubkey: Optional[str]) -> bool:\n28\t    \"\"\"True iff `payload` carries a valid owner signature over its declared core fields. Fail-closed.\"\"\"\n29\t    if not trusted_pubkey:\n30\t        return False\n31\t    sig = payload.get(\"sig\")\n32\t    if not sig or payload.get(\"pubkey\") != trusted_pubkey:\n33\t        return False\n34\t    core = {k: payload.get(k) for k in core_fields}\n35\t    return verify_one(trusted_pubkey, _canon(core), sig)\n36\t"}]},"uuid":"61b74477-fe5c-4f5c-9cb6-d3fecaaabba3","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:48.857Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"374208fa-2854-4152-a1d3-bba5304d7973","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"e28165e1-99e2-470c-8e85-c37f684ad42e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Uw6VzV5RWpgFhSPtuUMyQq","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SecretStore (Phase 7, WS-D D-ii) — keyring-first secret handling. Reads/writes API keys via the\n2\tOS keyring (macOS Keychain / Windows Credential Manager / libsecret) when the `keyring` package is\n3\tpresent; otherwise falls back to the plaintext `~/.sigil/sigil.env` that `config._load_env_file`\n4\talready loads. Secrets NEVER enter the append-only spine, a log, or a network payload — this is\n5\towner-local plumbing that keeps them off the plaintext file and off the wire (WS-D transport carries\n6\tonly `{seq,tier,kind}`).\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\timport os\n10\tfrom typing import Optional\n11\t\n12\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n13\t\n14\t_SERVICE = \"sigil\"\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tclass SecretStore:\n18\t    def __init__(self):\n19\t        try:\n20\t            import keyring\n21\t            self._kr = keyring\n22\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no keyring backend → env fallback only\n23\t            self._kr = None\n24\t\n25\t    @property\n26\t    def backend(self) -> str:\n27\t        return \"keyring\" if self._kr is not None else \"envfile\"\n28\t\n29\t    def get(self, key: str) -> Optional[str]:\n30\t        if self._kr is not None:\n31\t            try:\n32\t                v = self._kr.get_password(_SERVICE, key)\n33\t                if v is not None:\n34\t                    return v\n35\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n36\t                pass\n37\t        return os.environ.get(key)                    # sigil.env is loaded into env at config import\n38\t\n39\t    def set(self, key: str, value: str) -> str:\n40\t        \"\"\"Store a secret. Returns the backend used. Prefers the keyring; falls back to sigil.env\n41\t        (0600). Never writes the value anywhere else.\"\"\"\n42\t        if self._kr is not None:\n43\t            try:\n44\t                self._kr.set_password(_SERVICE, key, value)\n45\t                os.environ[key] = value               # make it live this process too\n46\t                return \"keyring\"\n47\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n48\t                pass\n49\t        self._env_upsert(key, value)\n50\t        os.environ[key] = value\n51\t        return \"envfile\"\n52\t\n53\t    @staticmethod\n54\t    def _env_upsert(key: str, value: str) -> None:\n55\t        f = SIGIL_HOME / \"sigil.env\"\n56\t        SIGIL_HOME.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n57\t        lines = []\n58\t        found = False\n59\t        try:\n60\t            for ln in f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines():\n61\t                if ln.split(\"=\", 1)[0].strip() == key:\n62\t                    lines.append(f\"{key}={value}\"); found = True\n63\t                else:\n64\t                    lines.append(ln)\n65\t        except OSError:\n66\t            pass\n67\t        if not found:\n68\t            lines.append(f\"{key}={value}\")\n69\t        # create with 0600 up-front (no world-readable window on first write) — N4\n70\t        fd = os.open(str(f), os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)\n71\t        with os.fdopen(fd, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fh:\n72\t            fh.write(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\")\n73\t"}]},"uuid":"7baa7b7e-619a-44f7-9c69-a01c70d9d619","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:06:49.404Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e28165e1-99e2-470c-8e85-c37f684ad42e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Proves the\n2\tdoctrine BY CONSTRUCTION: credentials never reach the append-only spine or the journal; no action fires\n3\twithout a verified owner approval bound to that exact step; a CAPTCHA/403 STOPS and is surfaced (never\n4\tdefeated, never a browser escalation); page/credential TOCTOU aborts; per-service creation cap; approval\n5\treplay refused; NO impersonation parameter exists; DELEGATE never promotes.\n6\tRun: ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python tests/test_actor.py\"\"\"\n7\timport tempfile\n8\tfrom pathlib import Path\n9\t\n10\timport sigil.agents.actor as actor_mod\n11\timport sigil.agents.actor_scope as scope_mod\n12\tfrom sigil.agents.actor import Delegate, WebStep\n13\tfrom sigil.agents.actor_scope import ActorScope\n14\tfrom sigil.agents.approvals import ApprovalQueue\n15\tfrom sigil.agents.base import Tier\n16\tfrom sigil.agents.vault import VaultRecord\n17\tfrom sigil.agents.web_engine import FakeEngine\n18\tfrom sigil.governor.promotion import NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS, PromotionPolicy\n19\tfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\n20\tfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n21\t\n22\tOWNER = generate_keypair()\n23\tOP = OWNER.public_key_b64\n24\t_KERNEL = Path(\"/home/kali/sigil/kernel/target/release/sigil-kernel\")\n25\t\n26\t# --- test isolation: journal to a temp dir (off the real ~/.sigil); \"public\" excludes private hosts ---\n27\t_TMP = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"actor-journal-\"))\n28\tactor_mod._ACTOR_HOME = _TMP\n29\tactor_mod._JOURNAL = _TMP / \"steps\"\n30\t_PRIVATE = {\"127.0.0.1\", \"localhost\", \"10.0.0.5\", \"169.254.169.254\", \"::1\"}\n31\tscope_mod.is_public_host = lambda h: h.lower() not in _PRIVATE     # real DNS would flake; keep the gate meaningful\n32\t\n33\tSENTINEL = \"SENTINEL-PW-9f3a2b-UNIQUE-DO-NOT-LEAK\"\n34\tSVC = \"https://svc.example\"\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef _store():\n38\t    return SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tclass FakeCls:\n42\t    \"\"\"Deterministic stand-in for the Rust oracle: only a clean read-verb is A0; every actor verb → A3.\"\"\"\n43\t    def classify(self, tool):\n44\t        return Tier.A0 if tool == \"http.get\" else Tier.A3\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tclass FakeVault:\n48\t    \"\"\"In-memory vault double (no keyring, no manifest). `password` is held out-of-band, resolved late.\"\"\"\n49\t    def __init__(self):\n50\t        self._r, self._pw = {}, {}\n51\t\n52\t    def add(self, service, *, email=\"\", username=\"\", password=\"\", version=1):\n53\t        self._r[service] = VaultRecord(service=service, email=email, username=username,\n54\t                                       password_ref=f\"vault/{service}/password\", version=version)\n55\t        self._pw[service] = password\n56\t\n57\t    def get_record(self, service):\n58\t        return self._r.get(service)\n59\t\n60\t    def resolve_password(self, service):\n61\t        return self._pw.get(service)\n62\t\n63\t    def bump(self, service):                          # simulate a credential rotation (version++)\n64\t        r = self._r[service]\n65\t        self._r[service] = VaultRecord(service=r.service, email=r.email, username=r.username,\n66\t                                       password_ref=r.password_ref, version=r.version + 1)\n67\t\n68\t\n69\tdef _vault():\n70\t    v = FakeVault()\n71\t    v.add(\"svc\", email=\"me@owner.example\", username=\"owner\", password=SENTINEL)\n72\t    return v\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tdef _delegate(store, *, pages=None, allowed=(SVC,), cap=1, vault=None):\n76\t    engine = FakeEngine(pages if pages is not None else {f\"{SVC}/signup\": (200, \"<form>signup</form>\")})\n77\t    d = Delegate(store, scope=ActorScope(list(allowed), creation_cap=cap),\n78\t                 vault=vault or _vault(), engine=engine, classifier=FakeCls(), trusted_pubkey=OP)\n79\t    return d, engine\n80\t\n81\t\n82\tdef _acts(engine):\n83\t    return [c for c in engine.calls if c[0] == \"act\"]\n84\t\n85\t\n86\t# ---- G2 WARDEN honesty (real oracle) -------------------------------------------------------------\n87\tdef test_warden_locks_actor_verbs_a3():\n88\t    if not _KERNEL.exists():\n89\t        print(\"    (skip real oracle — kernel not built)\")\n90\t        return\n91\t    from sigil.agents.kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n92\t    kc = KernelClassifier(kernel_bin=str(_KERNEL))\n93\t    for verb in (\"account.create\", \"login\", \"submit.form\", \"purchase.item\", \"form.fill\", \"browser.navigate\"):\n94\t        assert kc.classify(verb) == Tier.A3, f\"{verb} must be A3 (explicit, per-action) by the real oracle\"\n95\t    assert kc.classify(\"http.get\") == Tier.A0, \"a clean read-verb is A0\"\n96\t\n97\t\n98\t# ---- impersonation impossible by CONSTRUCTION (absence of a code path, not a check) --------------\n99\tdef test_no_impersonation_field_exists():\n100\t    fields = set(WebStep.__dataclass_fields__)\n101\t    assert fields == {\"service\", \"kind\", \"url\", \"fields\"}, f\"WebStep has no identity/impersonate field: {fields}\"\n102\t    for banned in (\"as_identity\", \"impersonate\", \"on_behalf_of\", \"actor\", \"subject_identity\"):\n103\t        assert banned not in fields\n104\t\n105\t\n106\tdef test_delegate_never_promotes():\n107\t    assert \"DELEGATE\" in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS, \"DELEGATE must be structurally no-promotion (like ENVOY)\"\n108\t    s = _store()\n109\t    assert PromotionPolicy(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey=OP).grant(\"DELEGATE\", \"*\") is None, \\\n110\t        \"granting DELEGATE promotion is refused\"\n111\t    assert PromotionPolicy(s, trusted_pubkey=OP).is_promoted(\"DELEGATE\", \"*\") is False, \"DELEGATE is never promoted\"\n112\t\n113\t\n114\t# ---- scope: deny-all origin allowlist + SSRF (private host) --------------------------------------\n115\tdef test_offlist_origin_is_refused_no_request():\n116\t    s = _store()\n117\t    d, engine = _delegate(s, pages={\"https://evil.example/x\": (200, \"<html>ok</html>\")})\n118\t    queued, res = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", \"https://evil.example/x\", {\"u\": \"vault:username\"})])\n119\t    assert queued == [] and any(\"REFUSED\" in n for n in res.notes), \"an off-allowlist origin is refused\"\n120\t    assert not _acts(engine), \"a refused step performs no action\"\n121\t\n122\t\n123\tdef test_private_host_is_refused_ssrf():\n124\t    s = _store()\n125\t    d, engine = _delegate(s, allowed=(\"http://127.0.0.1\",),\n126\t                          pages={\"http://127.0.0.1/login\": (200, \"<form/>\")})\n127\t    queued, res = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", \"http://127.0.0.1/login\", {\"u\": \"vault:username\"})])\n128\t    assert queued == [] and any(\"REFUSED\" in n for n in res.notes), \"a private/loopback host is refused (SSRF)\"\n129\t    assert not _acts(engine)\n130\t\n131\t\n132\t# ---- the core approval gate: no approval → no request; approval → exactly one act ----------------\n133\tdef test_unapproved_step_makes_no_request():\n134\t    s = _store()\n135\t    d, engine = _delegate(s)\n136\t    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\",\n137\t                                   {\"user\": \"vault:username\", \"pw\": \"vault:password\"})])\n138\t    assert len(queued) == 1, \"the login step queues (A3)\"\n139\t    ex = d.execute(queued[0])                          # NO approval\n140\t    assert not ex.applied and any(\"no verified owner approval\" in n for n in ex.notes)\n141\t    assert not _acts(engine), \"an unapproved step performs ZERO requests\"\n142\t\n143\t\n144\tdef test_approved_step_executes_exactly_one_act():\n145\t    s = _store()\n146\t    d, engine = _delegate(s)\n147\t    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\",\n148\t                                   {\"user\": \"vault:username\", \"pw\": \"vault:password\"})])\n149\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(queued[0])\n150\t    ex = d.execute(queued[0])\n151\t    assert ex.applied, \"an approved step executes\"\n152\t    acts = _acts(engine)\n153\t    assert len(acts) == 1 and sorted(acts[0][2]) == [\"pw\", \"user\"], \"exactly one act, carrying the field NAMES only\"\n154\t\n155\t\n156\tdef test_credential_never_on_spine_or_journal():\n157\t    s = _store()\n158\t    d, engine = _delegate(s)\n159\t    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\",\n160\t                                   {\"user\": \"vault:username\", \"pw\": \"vault:password\"})])\n161\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(queued[0])\n162\t    d.execute(queued[0])\n163\t    spine_bytes = Path(s.path).read_text()\n164\t    assert SENTINEL not in spine_bytes, \"the password NEVER appears in the append-only spine\"\n165\t    journal_bytes = \"\".join(p.read_text() for p in (actor_mod._JOURNAL).glob(\"*.json\"))\n166\t    assert SENTINEL not in journal_bytes, \"the password NEVER appears in the 0700 step journal (only a vault reference)\"\n167\t    assert not any(SENTINEL in str(c) for c in engine.calls), \"the password NEVER appears in a recorded engine call\"\n168\t\n169\t\n170\t# ---- blocks are surfaced, never defeated, never escalated ----------------------------------------\n171\tdef test_captcha_or_403_at_preview_surfaces_and_never_queues():\n172\t    for status, html in ((403, \"\"), (200, \"<div>Please complete the reCAPTCHA</div>\"), (429, \"\")):\n173\t        s = _store()\n174\t        d, engine = _delegate(s, pages={f\"{SVC}/signup\": (status, html)})\n175\t        queued, res = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"account.create\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"u\": \"vault:username\"})])\n176\t        assert queued == [], f\"a blocked page ({status}) is NOT queued for action\"\n177\t        assert any(\"BLOCKED\" in n for n in res.notes)\n178\t        assert any(r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"web.actor.blocked\" for r in s.iter_records()), \"the block is surfaced\"\n179\t        assert not _acts(engine), \"a block is never acted on (never defeated)\"\n180\t\n181\t\n182\tdef test_block_appearing_at_execute_stops_before_acting():\n183\t    s = _store()\n184\t    d, engine = _delegate(s)                            # good page at preview\n185\t    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"pw\": \"vault:password\"})])\n186\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(queued[0])\n187\t    engine.pages[f\"{SVC}/signup\"] = (403, \"\")           # the site starts blocking AFTER approval\n188\t    ex = d.execute(queued[0])\n189\t    assert not ex.applied and any(\"BLOCKED at execute\" in n for n in ex.notes)\n190\t    assert not _acts(engine), \"a block detected at execute stops before any action\"\n191\t\n192\t\n193\t# ---- TOCTOU: a changed page OR a rotated credential invalidates the approval ----------------------\n194\tdef test_page_change_between_approval_and_execute_aborts():\n195\t    s = _store()\n196\t    d, engine = _delegate(s)\n197\t    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"pw\": \"vault:password\"})])\n198\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(queued[0])\n199\t    engine.pages[f\"{SVC}/signup\"] = (200, \"<form>DIFFERENT PAGE</form>\")   # page changed\n200\t    ex = d.execute(queued[0])\n201\t    assert not ex.applied and any(\"token mismatch\" in n for n in ex.notes), \"a page change aborts (anti-TOCTOU)\"\n202\t    assert not _acts(engine)\n203\t\n204\t\n205\tdef test_credential_rotation_between_approval_and_execute_aborts():\n206\t    s = _store()\n207\t    v = _vault()\n208\t    d, engine = _delegate(s, vault=v)\n209\t    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"pw\": \"vault:password\"})])\n210\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(queued[0])\n211\t    v.bump(\"svc\")                                       # the owner rotated the password → version++\n212\t    ex = d.execute(queued[0])\n213\t    assert not ex.applied and any(\"token mismatch\" in n for n in ex.notes), \\\n214\t        \"a credential rotation (version bump) invalidates the approval — re-approval required\"\n215\t    assert not _acts(engine)\n216\t\n217\t\n218\tdef test_approval_of_one_step_cannot_authorize_another():\n219\t    s = _store()\n220\t    d, engine = _delegate(s)\n221\t    step = WebStep(\"svc\", \"login\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"pw\": \"vault:password\"})\n222\t    queued, _ = d.preview([step, step])                # two identical steps → seqs A and B (same token)\n223\t    a, b = queued\n224\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(a)\n225\t    assert not d.execute(b).applied, \"an approval bound to seq A cannot authorize seq B (replay refused)\"\n226\t    assert not _acts(engine), \"the replayed step performs no action\"\n227\t    assert d.execute(a).applied and len(_acts(engine)) == 1, \"the genuinely-approved step executes once\"\n228\t\n229\t\n230\t# ---- mass-creation is out of doctrine: per-service cap -------------------------------------------\n231\tdef test_per_service_creation_cap():\n232\t    s = _store()\n233\t    d, engine = _delegate(s, cap=1)\n234\t    q1, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"account.create\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"u\": \"vault:username\"})])\n235\t    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(q1[0])\n236\t    assert d.execute(q1[0]).applied, \"the first account.create is allowed\"\n237\t    q2, res = d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\", \"account.create\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"u\": \"vault:username\"})])\n238\t    assert q2 == [] and any(\"creation cap\" in n for n in res.notes), \"a second account.create is refused (cap=1)\"\n239\t\n240\t\n241\t# ---- construction: the actor has NO browser/escalation path (block-before-escalate is vacuous) ---\n242\tdef test_actor_has_no_browser_escalation_path():\n243\t    src = Path(actor_mod.__file__).read_text()\n244\t    # no browser/JS-render/escalation call path (impersonation is covered structurally by the field test)\n245\t    for banned in (\"BrowserEngine\", \"needs_js\", \"playwright\", \"escalate\", \".act(\", \"import subprocess\"):\n246\t        if banned == \".act(\":\n247\t            assert src.count(\".act(\") == 1, \"exactly one engine.act call site (the single, gated action)\"\n248\t            continue\n249\t        assert banned not in src, f\"the actor must contain no {banned!r} path (HTTP-only, no evasion surface)\"\n250\t\n251\t\n252\t# ---- the REAL HttpEngine independently refuses a private host (defense-in-depth below ActorScope) --\n253\tdef test_real_http_engine_refuses_private_host_act():\n254\t    from sigil.agents.web_engine import HttpEngine\n255\t    out = HttpEngine().act({\"url\": \"http://127.0.0.1/login\", \"kind\": \"login\"}, {\"pw\": SENTINEL})\n256\t    assert out.get(\"ok\") is False and out.get(\"reason\") == \"ssrf-refused\", \\\n257\t        \"the real engine's own _vetted_ip gate refuses a loopback POST — no egress even if scope were bypassed\"\n258\t\n259\t\n260\tif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n261\t    fns = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith(\"test_\")]\n262\t    passed = 0\n263\t    for fn in fns:\n264\t        try:\n265\t            fn(); print(f\"  PASS  {fn.__name__}\"); passed += 1\n266\t        except AssertionError as e:\n267\t            print(f\"  FAIL  {fn.__name__}: {e}\")\n268\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n269\t            import traceback\n270\t            print(f\"  ERROR {fn.__name__}: {e}\")\n271\t            traceback.print_exc()\n272\t    print(f\"{passed}/{len(fns)} Phase-8 WS-G (DELEGATE) doctrine guarantees One batched preview of K identical\naccount.create steps + K owner approvals => K accounts created despite creation_cap=1.\n\"\"\"\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport sigil.agents.actor as actor_mod\nimport sigil.agents.actor_scope as scope_mod\nfrom sigil.agents.actor import Delegate, WebStep\nfrom sigil.agents.actor_scope import ActorScope\nfrom sigil.agents.approvals import ApprovalQueue\nfrom sigil.agents.base import Tier\nfrom sigil.agents.vault import VaultRecord\nfrom sigil.agents.web_engine import FakeEngine\nfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n\nOWNER = generate_keypair()\nOP = OWNER.public_key_b64\n\n_TMP = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"repro-cap-\"))\nactor_mod._ACTOR_HOME = _TMP\nactor_mod._JOURNAL = _TMP / \"steps\"\n_PRIVATE = {\"127.0.0.1\", \"localhost\", \"10.0.0.5\", \"169.254.169.254\", \"::1\"}\nscope_mod.is_public_host = lambda h: h.lower() not in _PRIVATE\n\nSVC = \"https://svc.example\"\n\n\nclass FakeCls:\n    def classify(self, tool):\n        return Tier.A0 if tool == \"http.get\" else Tier.A3\n\n\nclass FakeVault:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self._r, self._pw = {}, {}\n    def add(self, service, *, email=\"\", username=\"\", password=\"\", version=1):\n        self._r[service] = VaultRecord(service=service, email=email, username=username,\n                                       password_ref=f\"vault/{service}/password\", version=version)\n        self._pw[service] = password\n    def get_record(self, service):\n        return self._r.get(service)\n    def resolve_password(self, service):\n        return self._pw.get(service)\n\n\ndef main():\n    store = SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\n    vault = FakeVault()\n    vault.add(\"svc\", email=\"me@owner.example\", username=\"owner\", password=\"PW\")\n    engine = FakeEngine({f\"{SVC}/signup\": (200, \"<form>signup</form>\")})\n    scope = ActorScope([SVC], creation_cap=1)          # CAP = 1\n    d = Delegate(store, scope=scope, vault=vault, engine=engine,\n                 classifier=FakeCls(), trusted_pubkey=OP)\n\n    K = 3\n    step = WebStep(\"svc\", \"account.create\", f\"{SVC}/signup\", {\"u\": \"vault:username\"})\n    # ONE batched preview of K identical account.create steps (all see count==0 => all queue)\n    queued, res = d.preview([step] * K)\n    print(f\"preview: {len(queued)} of {K} account.create steps QUEUED (cap=1). notes={res.notes}\")\n\n    q = ApprovalQueue(store, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP)\n    for seq in queued:\n        q.approve(seq)\n\n    applied = 0\n    for seq in queued:\n        ex = d.execute(seq)\n        if ex.applied:\n            applied += 1\n    acts = [c for c in engine.calls if c[0] == \"act\"]\n    print(f\"execute: {applied} of {K} steps APPLIED; {len(acts)} real engine.act() calls (POSTs)\")\n\n    # count of committed account.create 'applied' records for svc\n    n = sum(1 for r in store.iter_records()\n            if r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"web.actor.step\"\n            and r.payload.get(\"step_kind\") == \"account.create\"\n            and r.payload.get(\"service\") == \"svc\"\n            and r.payload.get(\"status\") == \"applied\")\n    print(f\"spine now holds {n} APPLIED account.create records for one service (cap was 1)\")\n    print(f\"scope.creation_allowed AFTER the fact: {scope.creation_allowed(store, 'svc')} \"\n          f\"(execute never consulted it)\")\n    print()\n    if applied > scope.creation_cap:\n        print(f\"*** CAP BYPASS CONFIRMED: created {applied} accounts with creation_cap={scope.creation_cap} ***\")\n    else:\n        print(\"cap held\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":14151,"cache_read_input_tokens":85169,"output_tokens":2012,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":14151},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":2012,"cache_read_input_tokens":85169,"cache_creation_input_tokens":14151,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":14151,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHemK7uQxiKr9gesKjo","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"d1763699-9ed0-4992-93c3-d6df4bb12c43","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:10:36.814Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"ed88d448-c3ad-4d86-b9ae-e8689e51d986","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9afb2a14d425ea77","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAHjeENHKEizaSk7uc7j","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Hz3BDVv9e7fpUQWDQZJkZm","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/repro_replay.py","content":"\"\"\"Repro: a SINGLE owner approval authorizes UNBOUNDED repeated execution.\nexecute(seq) has no idempotency guard and never retires the approval, so calling it\nN times on ONE approved step fires N real actions. 'Per-action owner approval' broken.\nDemonstrated with purchase.item (financial) and account.create (also => cap bypass with ONE approval).\n\"\"\"\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport sigil.agents.actor as actor_mod\nimport sigil.agents.actor_scope as scope_mod\nfrom sigil.agents.actor import Delegate, WebStep\nfrom sigil.agents.actor_scope import ActorScope\nfrom sigil.agents.approvals import ApprovalQueue\nfrom sigil.agents.base import Tier\nfrom sigil.agents.vault import VaultRecord\nfrom sigil.agents.web_engine import FakeEngine\nfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n\nOWNER = generate_keypair(); OP = OWNER.public_key_b64\n_TMP = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"repro-replay-\"))\nactor_mod._ACTOR_HOME = _TMP; actor_mod._JOURNAL = _TMP / \"steps\"\nscope_mod.is_public_host = lambda h: True\nSVC = \"https://shop.example\"\n\n\nclass FakeCls:\n    def classify(self, tool): return Tier.A0 if tool == \"http.get\" else Tier.A3\n\nclass FakeVault:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self._r = {\"shop\": VaultRecord(service=\"shop\", email=\"me@x.example\", username=\"owner\",\n                                       password_ref=\"vault/shop/password\", version=1)}\n        self._pw = {\"shop\": \"PW\"}\n    def get_record(self, s): return self._r.get(s)\n    def resolve_password(self, s): return self._pw.get(s)\n\n\ndef run(kind, cap):\n    store = SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\n    engine = FakeEngine({f\"{SVC}/buy\": (200, \"<form>buy</form>\")})\n    d = Delegate(store, scope=ActorScope([SVC], creation_cap=cap), vault=FakeVault(),\n                 engine=engine, classifier=FakeCls(), trusted_pubkey=OP)\n    queued, _ = d.preview([WebStep(\"shop\", kind, f\"{SVC}/buy\", {\"card\": \"vault:username\"})])\n    assert len(queued) == 1\n    seq = queued[0]\n    ApprovalQueue(store, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(seq)   # ONE approval\n\n    fires = 0\n    for _ in range(5):\n        if d.execute(seq).applied:          # SAME seq, over and over\n            fires += 1\n    acts = [c for c in engine.calls if c[0] == \"act\"]\n    print(f\"kind={kind:<15} cap={cap}  ONE approval -> execute(seq)x5 -> \"\n          f\"{fires} APPLIED, {len(acts)} real engine.act() POSTs\")\n\n\nprint(\"=== approval replay: one owner signature, many actions ===\")\nrun(\"purchase.item\", cap=1)\nrun(\"account.create\", cap=1)\nprint(\"\\n*** One owner approval fires the action as many times as execute(seq) is called ***\")\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":485,"cache_read_input_tokens":103479,"output_tokens":3235,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":485},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3235,"cache_read_input_tokens":103479,"cache_creation_input_tokens":485,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":485,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAHjbVfBU82E46ofJw4o","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"3d17b511-05d3-486e-b592-2d3577cfc7d6","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:12:05.189Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
